### [100] [On intrinsic homological mirror symmetry for toric degenerations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21720)
*Shaoyun Bai,Sebastian Haney*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 研究极大单幂退化 Calabi–Yau 流形的同调镜像对称，通过 monodromy 迭代的固定点 Floer 上同调与 pair of pants 乘积构造一个环，在可交换性假设下用 relative Proj 构造候选镜像族，并在存在 tropical Lagrangian section 时给出从镜像族上完美复形导出范畴到纤维 Fukaya 范畴的满忠实嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Perutz、Siebert 的提议及 Gross–Siebert 内蕴镜像对称纲领启发，旨在刻画同调镜像对称的 Floer 理论侧面，并把证明同调镜像对称的辛几何输入尽量约化为构造 tropical Lagrangian section 的问题。

Method: 对 punctured disk 上光滑射影 Calabi–Yau 流形的极大单幂退化，取 monodromy 各次迭代的固定点 Floer 上同调群，配备 pair of pants 乘积构造一个环；假设该环可交换，用 relative Proj 构造候选镜像族；再假设光滑纤维 X_t 含 tropical Lagrangian section，构造导出范畴嵌入。

Result: 在环可交换与存在 tropical Lagrangian section 两个假设下，构造了从候选镜像族上完美复形的导出范畴到 X_t 的 Fukaya 范畴的满忠实嵌入；并在若干 Batyrev–Borisov toric 退化以及由 Gross–Siebert 重构算法得到的 Calabi–Yau 三维流形 toric 退化上验证了这两个假设。

Conclusion: 两个几何假设均被设计为支持极大单幂退化 Calabi–Yau 流形镜像对称的一般研究，将证明同调镜像对称的辛几何输入大幅约化为构造 tropical Lagrangian section 的问题。

Abstract: This paper studies the Floer-theoretic aspects of homological mirror symmetry inspired by proposals of Perutz and Siebert and the Gross--Siebert intrinsic mirror symmetry program. Given a maximally unipotent degeneration of smooth projective Calabi--Yau manifolds over the punctured disk, we construct a ring using the fixed point Floer cohomology groups of the iterates of the monodromy of the degeneration equipped with the pair of pants product. Under the assumption that this ring is commutative, we can consider a candidate mirror family defined by the relative Proj construction. Further assuming that a smooth fiber $X_t$ contains a so-called tropical Lagrangian section, we construct a fully faithful embedding from the derived category of perfect complexes on our candidate mirror family into the Fukaya category of $X_t$. We verify both of these assumptions for certain Batyrev--Borisov toric degenerations, as well as some toric degenerations of Calabi--Yau threefolds coming from the Gross--Siebert reconstruction algorithm. These two geometric hypotheses are both phrased to support the general study of mirror symmetry for maximally unipotent degenerations of Calabi--Yau manifolds, largely reducing the symplectic inputs for proving homological mirror symmetry to the problem of constructing tropical Lagrangian sections.

</details>


### [101] [Periodic Magnetic Geodesics with Every Low Energy: Existence and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21931)
*Valerio Assenza,Gabriele Benedetti,Leonardo Macarini*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 在非紧、非完备的黎曼流形上，若磁场强度在某非空紧集上取得严格局部极大，则每个充分低的能级都携带靠近该集、长度随能量趋于零的可收缩周期磁测地线；紧流形情形下每个充分小能级亦然。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非紧、非完备黎曼流形上闭2-形式磁场中低能级周期磁测地线的存在性，放宽先前工作对紧性、完备性与磁场恰当性的假设，并揭示磁场强度严格局部极大这一几何条件下能量的低能级谱行为。

Method: 采用拉格朗日作用泛函的变分法：针对β非恰当，将极小极大限制在短环集合上；针对g非完备，结合流形紧化、Thom喷流横截性以及能量趋于零的磁测地线序列的爆破分析；针对Palais–Smale列可能非紧，利用低能下Ricci磁曲率的正性；摒弃Struwe单调性论证，改用Abbondandolo–Majer的双Lyapunov函数论证。

Result: 若磁场强度的严格局部极大点集K非空且紧，则存在靠近K、长度随能量趋于零的可收缩周期磁测地线，且其出现在K的任意邻域中；当Q紧时，每个充分小能级均携带可收缩周期磁测地线。此外证明一般情况下K的非空性与紧性均不可省略。

Conclusion: 磁场强度的严格局部极大构成低能级周期磁测地线的产生机制，该结论在非紧非完备情形下依然成立；所发展的短环极小极大、紧化-横截-爆破及双Lyapunov函数技术为处理非恰当磁场与非完备度量的变分问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $(Q,g)$ be a Riemannian manifold equipped with a non-identically zero magnetic field represented by a closed $2$-form $β$. We allow $Q$ to be non-compact and do not assume that the metric $g$ is complete. We prove that if the magnetic strength, defined as the pointwise norm of $β$, attains a strict local maximum on a non-empty compact set $K$, then every sufficiently low energy level carries a contractible periodic magnetic geodesic of the pair $(g,β)$ localized near $K$. More precisely, such orbits exist in every neighborhood of $K$, and their lengths converge to zero with the energy. In particular, if $Q$ is compact, then every sufficiently small energy level carries a contractible periodic magnetic geodesic. We also show that, in general, neither the non-emptiness nor the compactness of $K$ can be omitted.
  Our proof relies on the calculus of variations of the Lagrangian action functional, and uses several new ideas. More precisely, we overcome: (i) the non-exactness of $β$ by restricting the minimax to the set of short loops; (ii) the non-completeness of $g$ by combining a compactification of $Q$ with Thom's Jet Transversality and a blow-up for sequences of magnetic geodesics with energy tending to zero; (iii) the possible non-compactness of Palais--Smale sequences by the positivity of the Ricci magnetic curvature for low energy established by the first-named author. Unlike previous work, Struwe's monotonicity argument cannot be used for our purposes, and we rely on a two-Lyapunov-function argument due to Abbondandolo and Majer.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Slowly Rotating Wormholes in f(R,T) Gravity: Particle Dynamics and Weak-field Lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20896)
*M. Sharif,Tayyab Naseer,Rida Batool*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 f(R,T) 引力框架下构建慢旋转可穿越虫洞模型，证明其无需奇异物质即可满足零能量与强能量条件，并研究粒子轨道、拖曳效应与弱场透镜特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中可穿越虫洞几乎总需要违反零能量条件的奇异物质，因此作者希望借助含物质-几何耦合的修正引力理论，寻找更物理、更可行的虫洞支撑机制。

Method: 在 f(R,T) 理论中构造慢旋转、稳态的可穿越虫洞解，耦合各向异性流体，检验正则性、无事件视界、渐近平直与喉部 flare-out 条件，并分析能量条件、粒子运动、参考系拖曳、非测地运动以及弱场与透镜效应。

Result: 所得虫洞模型正则、无视界、远处趋于平直、喉部满足 flare-out 要求；所需物质同时满足零能量条件和强能量条件，说明旋转虫洞不必然需要非传统物质源。

Conclusion: 慢旋转可穿越虫洞在 f(R,T) 引力中具有潜在的天体物理可行性，为无需奇异物质的旋转虫洞提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Among all gravitational solutions, traversable wormholes stand out as truly fascinating, yet within general relativity, they almost always demand exotic matter that necessarily violate null energy requirement. The inclusion of matter-geometry coupling in modified gravity theories provides a viable platform where wormholes remain open which are supported by effective gravitational field. We therefore examine the structure and properties of slowly rotating traversable wormholes using the $f(R,T)$ framework. We formulate stationary wormhole models coupled with the anisotropic fluid and show that they are regular, horizon-free, approach a flat spacetime geometry at large distances, and satisfy the flare-out requirement at the throat. Our results indicate that the required matter meets both the null and strong energy conditions, demonstrating that rotating wormholes do not necessarily require unconventional sources. We further study particle trajectories, frame dragging phenomena, and non-geodesic behavior resulting from matter-geometry coupling, alongside the weak-field and lensing effects caused by the rotation. Our results establish the potential astrophysical viability of rotating wormholes in $f(R,T)$ gravity.

</details>


### [103] [Thermodynamic topology of magnetic GMGHS black holes: the $\overline {W}^{1-}$ subclass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20898)
*Dejiang Yin,Qi-Qi Liang,Yu-Die Wan,Li-Yun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维渐近平直Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton引力中带电非极端磁Gibbons–Maeda族的 thermodynamic topology，重点分析弦论成员GMGHS黑洞（a=1），给出逆Hawking温度的全域解析分类，并揭示W数不足以完全刻画热力学拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究提出全局拓扑数W用于分类黑洞热力学拓扑，但W是否足以完全刻画热力学拓扑（尤其在同一W下区分不同子类）尚不明确，需在渐近平直dilaton解中寻找具体实现与判据。

Method: 对四维渐近平直Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton引力中带电非极端磁Gibbons–Maeda族，在正则外视界完整定义域内，对逆Hawking温度进行解析分类；重点考察dilaton耦合a=1的GMGHS黑洞，分析defect curve及其渐近行为。

Result: dilaton耦合将该族分为三类逆Hawking温度极限结构。a=1时GMGHS defect curve趋于非零的下逆温度端点，且仅含单一不稳定分支，从而在渐近平直Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton解中显式实现了此前提出的W̄^{1-}热力学拓扑子类。

Conclusion: 全局拓扑数W不能完全刻画黑洞热力学拓扑；物理视界域边界附近逆Hawking温度的渐近行为可作为区分相同W下不同热力学拓扑子类的附加判据。

Abstract: Thermodynamic topology provides a topological classification of black hole states and their branch structures in thermodynamic parameter space. We investigate the thermodynamic topology of the charged nonextremal magnetic Gibbons--Maeda family in four-dimensional asymptotically flat Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton gravity, with particular emphasis on its string-theory member at the dilaton coupling $a=1$, the Gibbons--Maeda--Garfinkle--Horowitz--Strominger (GMGHS) black hole. An analytic classification over the complete domain of regular outer horizons shows that the dilaton coupling separates the family into three limiting structures of the inverse Hawking temperature. At $a=1$, the GMGHS defect curve approaches a nonzero lower inverse temperature endpoint and contains a single unstable branch. In particular, the magnetic GMGHS black hole provides an explicit realization of the previously proposed $\overline W^{1-}$ thermodynamic topological subclass within an asymptotically flat Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton solution. This result demonstrates that the global topological number $W$ alone does not completely characterize black hole thermodynamic topology. The asymptotic behavior of the inverse Hawking temperature near the boundaries of the physical horizon domain provides an additional criterion for distinguishing thermodynamic topological subclasses with the same $W$.

</details>


### [104] [Second-order scattering response of a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20934)
*Bruno Bucciotti,Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Adrien Kuntz,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过黑洞微扰论计算引力波在黑洞附近的非线性耦合，发现耦合在驱动频率等于基模准正规模频率时达到峰值，且在最大允许谐波处最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波在黑洞附近呈非线性耦合，需要量化这种耦合的大小及其对宇称成分、频率和角结构的依赖关系。

Method: 应用黑洞微扰论计算引力波的非线性耦合，并分析其对宇称成分、频率与角结构的依赖；低频频段将二次响应解释为黑洞时空的非线性动力学潮汐形变，并与5点Compton引力子散射振幅对比。

Result: 非线性耦合在驱动频率匹配基模准正规模振荡频率时普遍达到峰值；激发在最大允许谐波处最强，对入射模的宇称成分依赖较弱；二次黑洞耦合系数随驱动频率呈二次标度，该标度可由5点Compton引力子散射振幅重现。

Conclusion: 黑洞引力波的非线性耦合由准正规模共振主导，低频二次响应对应黑洞时空本身的非线性动力学潮汐形变，其频率二次标度与散射振幅结果一致。

Abstract: Gravitational waves couple in a nonlinear fashion in the vicinity of a black hole. Black hole perturbation theory can be readily applied to compute the magnitude of this coupling, its dependence on the parity content, frequencies, and angular structure of the incoming gravitational waves. In this work we carry out this calculation, showing how the nonlinear coupling of gravitational waves generically peaks when the driven frequency matches the oscillation frequency of the fundamental quasinormal mode of the black hole. We also demonstrate how this excitation is largest at the maximal harmonics allowed, and that it only depends mildly on the parity content of the incoming modes. At low frequencies, the quadratic response computed here encodes the nonlinear, dynamical tidal deformability of the black hole spacetime itself. We demonstrate numerically that the quadratic black hole coupling coefficient scales quadratically with the driving frequency, and recover this scaling from the 5-point Compton graviton scattering amplitude.

</details>


### [105] [Zero Temperature, Degenerate Fermion Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20950)
*S. Boatto,M. B. Paranjape,V. Pessanha,C. A. D. Zarro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自洽方法描述零温退化费米气体在引力作用下形成量子星的过程，费米子按泡利原理占据由自引力决定的能级，最高占据能级决定星体半径。牛顿极限下得到简谐振子势并解析求解任意半径和质量的星体；广义相对论框架下可独立恢复Buchdahl界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仅通过引力自相互作用的退化费米气体星的形成机制，探索自洽的量子力学描述方法，并检验牛顿极限下能否构造比Buchdahl界限更致密甚至小于史瓦西半径的星体。

Method: 采用自洽方法：费米子按泡利原理占据由自引力相互作用决定的能级。先在牛顿引力极限下分析，发现费米子感受简谐振子势且外部光滑衔接1/r牛顿势，从而解析求解。再扩展到广义相对论，采用内部Schwarzschild解与外部Schwarzschild度规光滑衔接、对物质作半经典处理。

Result: 牛顿极限下解析得到以费米子质量m和总数N_s为参数、任意半径和质量的星体解；可构造比Buchdahl界限更致密甚至小于史瓦西半径的星体（但已超出牛顿极限适用范围）。广义相对论框架下独立恢复了Buchdahl界限。

Conclusion: 该自洽能级方法为退化费米星形成提供了新视角，牛顿极限解虽可突破Buchdahl界限但需广义相对论修正；广义相对论分析验证了Buchdahl界限的普适性。简要讨论了牛顿解经典稳定性及其与牛顿多方流体的关系。

Abstract: We study a degenerate, zero temperature, fermion gas with rest mass $m$, that only interacts with itself, gravitationally. We describe a self-consistent novel approach to the formation of a degenerate, quantum mechanical, star. In this approach, the fermions occupy the energy levels that are determined by the self consistent gravitational interactions, according to the Pauli exclusion principle. Then in this novel approach, the size of the highest occupied level determines the radius of the star. In the Newtonian gravitational limit, the fermions feel a potential corresponding to a simple harmonic well that smoothly attaches in the exterior to the usual $1/r$ Newtonian gravitational potential. We find analytically, solutions for stars with any radius and mass, parametrized by $m$ and $N_s$, the total number of fermions. In the Newtonian limit, we easily find that we can construct a star that is more compact than the Buchdahl bound and indeed can even be smaller than the Schwarzschild radius. Clearly, such cases are outside of the purview of the Newtonian limit. Therefore we expand our analysis to fermions in the general relativistic context, interacting gravitationally with a metric. We consider the metric corresponding to the interior Schwarzschild solution smoothly connecting to the usual exterior Schwarzschild metric. Treating matter semi-classically, we recover the Buchdahl bound in a completely independent analysis. We briefly discuss classical stability of the Newtonian solution, and its relation to Newtonian polytropic fluids.

</details>


### [106] [The logotropic dark fluid as an energy diffusion in unimodular gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21006)
*Miguel Cruz,Samuel Lepe*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文证明对数暗流体与含无压物质及对数能量扩散函数的一类耗散幺模引力严格等价，并给出其参数A的动力学解释及晚期病态行为的修正方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数暗流体作为统一暗物质与暗能量的单流体模型，其对数温度参数A的物理起源未被阐明，且模型存在晚期病态行为（幻影分支、负能量密度），需要动力学重解释与修正。

Method: 在同质背景层面建立对数暗流体与耗散幺模引力的精确对应，推导有效流体的绝热声速c_s²=A/ρ，分析其随尺度的演化，并引入单参数饱和扩散律。

Result: 绝热声速c_s²=A/ρ在a_s=a_M/2^{1/3}处达到光速；物质能量密度在a_M处归零并变号，正是模型变为幻影的尺度；由于ρ对应对数流体的焓密度ε+P，幻影分支即物质区带负能量密度、视界熵下降的分支。

Conclusion: 参数A是能量从物质向几何部门扩散的速率（Q̇=3AH）；单参数饱和扩散律可消除晚期病态同时保持低红移现象学。

Abstract: We show that the logotropic dark fluid, proposed as a single-fluid unification of dark matter and dark energy, is exactly dissipative unimodular gravity with pressureless matter and the logarithmic energy diffusion function $Q(a)=3A\ln a+Q_{c}$. The correspondence is exact at the level of the homogeneous background, rather than asymptotic, and introduces no parameters beyond those already present in either description. It provides a dynamical reinterpretation of the logotropic temperature $A$, whose origin was left open in the original proposal: up to the expansion rate, $A$ is the rate at which energy is diffused from matter into the geometric sector, $\dot Q=3AH$. The adiabatic sound speed of the effective fluid is $c_{s}^{2}=A/ρ$, where $ρ$ is the unimodular matter energy density, and reaches the speed of light at $a_{s}=a_{M}/2^{1/3}$; the matter energy density then vanishes and changes sign at $a_{M}$, which is precisely the scale factor at which the model is known to become phantom. Since $ρ$ coincides with the enthalpy density $ε+P$ of the logotropic fluid, the phantom branch is the branch on which the matter sector carries negative energy density and on which the apparent horizon entropy decreases. Finally, we show that a one-parameter saturating diffusion law removes these late-time pathologies while preserving the low-redshift phenomenology.

</details>


### [107] [Non-Conservative Mechanics in the Compact-Body Mass-Shell Model and its Waveform Structuring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21119)
*Noah M. MacKay*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将致密双星并合的EOB映射重新解释为等约化质量空心球壳的同步旋转与收缩，并在引入非保守力学后给出可用的解析引力波波形表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EOB采用"漏斗中的弹珠"图像描述致密双星并合，已有工作仅分析该模型的能量部分与辐射引力波；本文旨在推进几何波形建模，为后续计算与对比提供解析基础。

Method: 将致密双星并合建模为具有恒定约化质量度量的空心球壳的同步旋转与收缩，并合条件为球壳半径达到总质量半径；在先前玩具模型条件下发展的质量壳波形分析基础上，施加必要的非保守力学约束。

Result: 得到了可用的质量壳建模引力波波形的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 所给出的解析波形为后续计算工作奠定基础，可用于与现有及最先进模型进行比较。

Abstract: A novel reinterpretation on the effective one-body (EOB) mapping of compact-body binary (CBB) coalescence was previously considered. Instead of the conventional ``marble in a funnel" description of EOB, CBB coalescence is modeled as the concurrent rotation and contraction of a hollow shell with a constant reduced mass measure. Merger is achieved, therefore, when the shell radius reaches the total mass radius. While past works analyzed the energetics sector of the model and the radiated gravitational waves (GWs), this work considers the geometric waveform modeling aspect. More specifically, we mature the mass-shell waveform analysis initially discussed with toy-model conditions by imposing necessary non-conservative mechanics, leading to the analytical expression of usable mass-shell modeled GW-forms. This is to set the foundation for eventual computational endeavors with the analytical waveforms presented here, enabling a comparison against current and state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [108] [Granular Structure and Local de Broglie Wavelength in Fuzzy Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21135)
*Flavio Rosales-Infante,Alejandro Quiñonez-Guerrero,Iván Álvarez-Rios,Francisco S. Guzmán*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究通过动力学弛豫形成的模糊暗物质晕，将密度场分解为平滑球形成分与含干涉图样的残余密度，发现残余场功率谱存在明确主导颗粒尺度，并与局域德布罗意波长同量级且系统性偏小，德布罗意波长沿径向在核-晕过渡处达到最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊暗物质（FDM）晕中的干涉图样会产生颗粒状密度结构，但其特征尺度与局域速度场、德布罗意波长以及核-晕形态之间的定量联系尚不清晰，本文旨在建立这一联系。

Method: 通过动力学弛豫生成FDM晕，待其达到维里化的核-晕结构后，将密度场分离为平滑球形成分和包含FDM干涉图样的残余密度；计算残余场的功率谱以确定主导颗粒尺度，并利用密度流得到的速度场计算局域德布罗意波长，进行二者对比及径向行为分析。

Result: 残余密度场功率谱呈现明确定义的主导尺度，该尺度不同于总密度及平滑核-晕模型的特征尺度；颗粒尺度与局域德布罗意波长保持同量级，但系统性地小于德布罗意波长；德布罗意波长的径向分布不均匀，自核心向外递减，并在孤子核与外晕过渡处达到最小值。

Conclusion: 研究定量关联了FDM晕的密度涨落、局域速度场与核-晕形态，表明颗粒尺度由局域德布罗意波长决定并受核-晕内部结构调制。

Abstract: We study the granular structure that emerges in a Fuzzy Dark Matter (FDM) halo formed through kinetic relaxation. After the system reaches a virialized core-halo structure, we separate the density field into a smooth spherical component and a residual density that contains the interference pattern characteristic of FDM halos. The power spectrum of this residual field shows a well defined dominant scale, different from the characteristic scale of the total density and of the smooth core-halo model. We compare this granular scale with a local de Broglie wave-lenght calculated from the velocity field obtained from the density current of the system. We find that both scales remain of the same order of magnitude, with the granular scale systematically smaller than the de Broglie wavelength. The radial behavior of the de Broglie wavelength also reveals a non-uniform internal structure, it decreases from the core and reaches its minimum near the transition between the solitonic core and the outer halo. These results provide a quantitative connection between density fluctuations, the local velocity field, and the core-halo morphology of FDM halos.

</details>


### [109] [Exact DeWitt kernels in Lorentzian quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21585)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 通过在Lorentzian路径积分中仅对非奇异路径求和来实现DeWitt边界条件，给出DeWitt核的一般定义与构造规则，并对若干微超空间模型精确求出这些核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在微超空间量子宇宙学的Lorentzian路径积分中实现DeWitt边界条件（即仅对非奇异路径求和），需要一个普适的核定义与显式构造规则，并检验其在不同宇宙模型中的具体形式。

Method: 对正半轴做lapse积分得到Wheeler–DeWitt算符的Dirichlet格林函数；对整个实轴积分得到Dirichlet群平均核。由于微超空间作用量至多二次，所有lapse积分均以闭式完成，无需鞍点近似。对闭合宇宙（无宇宙学常数）、平直de Sitter宇宙，以及闭合与开放de Sitter宇宙（用Airy函数表示）分别构造核。

Result: 两类核在两端点之一位于奇点处时均消失；无宇宙学常数的闭合宇宙其整实轴核恒为零；平直de Sitter宇宙得到相应核；闭合与开放de Sitter宇宙的核以Airy函数表出。DeWitt条件作为奇点处边界条件，在半直线上选出自我伴随约束算符，并唯一确定核及其归一化。

Conclusion: DeWitt边界条件可借助对非奇异路径的Lorentzian路径积分严格实现，所定义的DeWitt核在半直线上自然对应于自我伴随约束算符的Dirichlet格林函数，且在具体微超空间模型中获得精确闭式解，并唯一固定归一化。

Abstract: The DeWitt boundary condition can be implemented in the Lorentzian path integral of minisuperspace quantum cosmology by summing only over non-singular paths. We give a general definition of the resulting DeWitt kernels, together with explicit rules for constructing them, and evaluate them exactly. The lapse integral over the positive half-line yields the Dirichlet Green function of the Wheeler--DeWitt operator, whereas the integral over the whole real line yields the Dirichlet group-averaging kernel; both vanish when either endpoint lies at the singularity. Since the minisuperspace actions are at most quadratic, all lapse integrals are performed in closed form without saddle-point approximations. We obtain the kernels for the closed universe without cosmological constant, where the whole-real-line kernel vanishes identically, for the flat de Sitter universe, and, in terms of Airy functions, for the closed and open de Sitter universes. Throughout, the DeWitt condition is imposed as the boundary condition at the singularity, which selects a self-adjoint constraint operator on the half-line and fixes the kernels uniquely, including their normalization.

</details>


### [110] [Massive scalar's self-mass from gravitons in de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21750)
*Dražen Glavan,Shun-Pei Miao,Tomislav Prokopec,Richard P. Woodard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文计算了de Sitter空间中大质量标量场自能的一圈引力子圈图贡献，采用维度正规化并用BPHZ抵消项吸收发散，该自能可用于研究暴涨期间大质量旁观标量场动力学的单圈量子引力修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究暴涨时期大质量旁观标量场动力学中的单圈量子引力修正，需要一个完全重整化的自能结果作为基础。

Method: 采用维度正规化（dimensional regularization）计算de Sitter空间中大质量标量场自能的一圈引力子贡献，并由BPHZ抵消项吸收发散，得到完全重整化的自能。

Result: 获得了de Sitter空间内大质量标量场自能的一圈引力子贡献的完全重整化结果。

Conclusion: 该重整化自能可作为研究暴涨中大质量旁观标量场单圈量子引力修正的工具。

Abstract: We compute the fully renormalized one-graviton-loop contribution to the self-mass of a massive scalar field in de Sitter space. The computation is performed using dimensional regularization and the divergences are absorbed by BPHZ counterterms. This self-mass can be used to study one-loop quantum-gravitational corrections to the dynamics of massive spectator scalars in inflation.

</details>


### [111] [Revisiting the Entangled Relativity gravity framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21764)
*Bertrand Chauvineau*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将纠缠相对论嵌入更广泛的标量-张量理论框架，提出"内在解耦"条件，构造满足该条件的完美流体拉格朗日量，并在弱场球对称恒星中验证其作为广义相对论替代方案的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视纠缠相对论理论框架，将其嵌入一组更大的理论集合中，以评估其作为广义相对论替代方案的合理性与适用性。

Method: 通过标量-张量重构，引入"内在解耦"条件（即物质满足该条件时，理论允许具有相同物质内容的广义相对论时空解）；刻画满足该条件的物质拉格朗日量的特征（由其依赖度规张量的方式完全决定）；构造在1PN阶满足内在解耦条件的完美流体拉格朗日量；并将其应用于弱场球对称恒星。

Result: 数值计算得到的Eddington参数突然满足已知观测约束，表明纠缠相对论及其推广是广义相对论的有力替代方案。

Conclusion: 该理论在弱场球对称恒星情形下展现出与观测相符的潜力，但理论不完全严格遵守守恒律，因此需重新审视观测数据与理论参数之间的联系，才能对该问题作出最终澄清。

Abstract: One revisits the Entangled Relativity theory framework, embedding it in a larger set of theories. From their scalar-tensor reformulation, these theories admit General Relativity spacetime solutions as solutions with the same matter content, if this matter obeys an "intrinsic decoupling" condition. One shows that a set of matter Lagrangians satisfying this condition is completely characterized by the way they depend on the metric tensor. One then shows that it is possible to define a perfect fluid Lagrangian fulfilling the intrinsic decoupling condition up to 1PN order. Specifying to weak field spherical stars suggests that Entangled Relativity and its proposed generalization are worthy alternatives to General Relativity. Indeed, the obtained numerical values of the Eddington parameters abruptly satisfy the known observational constraints. However, the non strict compliance of the theory with conservation laws demands to reconsider the link between observational data and the parameters for a definitive clarification of this point.

</details>


### [112] [A computer assisted existence proof for a non-Schwarzschild black hole in Einstein--Weyl gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21797)
*Kevin Goldstein,Vishnu Jejjala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文用计算机辅助方法严格证明了四维Einstein–Weyl引力中存在一个非Schwarzschild静态渐近平坦黑洞解，其无量纲视界半径在自旋二质量标度下为1，参数接近已有数值分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Schwarzschild黑洞解此前仅由数值打靶法构造，缺乏严格的数学存在性证明，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将外部区域分解为收敛的视界级数域、由带方向舍入的参数相关Taylor模型包围的紧核心区，以及作为中心稳定流形不动点问题处理的无穷渐近域；构造五维匹配映射并验证其在严格给定的箱上连续，经精确有理预条件后利用Poincaré–Miranda定理的符号条件证明匹配零点存在。

Result: 证明了该解具有正则非退化视界、无额外外部视界、Ricci标量为零而Ricci张量非零，匹配零点存在但不唯一。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了非Schwarzschild黑洞解的计算机辅助存在性证明，验证了先前数值结果，但未建立匹配零点的唯一性。

Abstract: Static, asymptotically flat, non-Schwarzschild black holes in four-dimensional Einstein--Weyl gravity have previously been constructed by numerical shooting, while the local field equations admit horizon and asymptotic expansions to all orders. We give a computer assisted existence proof for a non-Schwarzschild solution whose dimensionless horizon radius is one in units of the massive spin-two scale, with parameters close to the previously reported numerical branch. The exterior is divided into a convergent horizon series domain, a compact core enclosed by a parameter dependent Taylor model with directed rounding, and an infinite asymptotic domain treated as a centered stable manifold fixed point problem. The resulting five-dimensional matching map between the core and tail is continuous on a rigorously specified box. After exact rational preconditioning, the signs on opposite faces satisfy the hypotheses of the Poincaré--Miranda theorem and therefore certify a matching zero. The solution has a regular nondegenerate horizon, no additional exterior horizon, vanishing Ricci scalar, and nonvanishing Ricci tensor. The proof establishes existence, but not uniqueness of the matching zero.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum Tunneling and the Emergence of Classical Spacetime in Einstein-Æther Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21814)
*Germano A. Monerat,Alessandro O. Castro Junior,Eduardo V. Corrêa Silva,Gil O. Neto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在Einstein-Æther引力框架下研究正曲率FLRW宇宙（含无压尘埃与正宇宙学常数Λ）的量子宇宙学模型，经经典哈密顿分析、Dirac量子化与数值求解，用WKB、多世界期望值与Bohm轨迹分析隧穿概率，发现Λ、σ、E较大而β较小时宇宙量子诞生的概率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Einstein-Æther引力这一修改引力理论在量子宇宙学中的表现，特别是早期宇宙的量子隧穿诞生过程如何受该理论基本参数影响。

Method: 采用Schutz变分形式做经典哈密顿分析，构建相空间并导出标度因子动力学；按Dirac形式量子化得到Wheeler-DeWitt方程并数值求解；用WKB近似计算隧穿概率TP_WKB与积分隧穿概率TP_int，结合多世界诠释下的标度因子期望值和Bohm轨迹进行分析。

Result: 识别出可导致暴胀膨胀的特定初始条件（直接源自量子动力学）；分析隧穿概率随理论参数（Λ、σ、β）及尘埃流体内能E的变化：Λ、σ、E取值较大且β较小时，宇宙量子诞生概率更高。

Conclusion: Einstein-Æther引力下的量子宇宙学模型存在有利于宇宙量子诞生的参数区间，隧穿概率对基本参数具有显著依赖性。

Abstract: We investigate a quantum cosmological model within the framework of Einstein-Æther gravity. The model consists of a positively curved FLRW Universe whose matter content is described by pressureless dust, in the presence of a positive cosmological constant $Λ$. We first perform a classical Hamiltonian analysis utilizing Schutz's variational formalism, constructing the phase space and deriving the dynamics of the scale factor. Notably, we identify specific initial conditions that lead to an inflationary expansion, which are extracted directly from the underlying quantum dynamics. Subsequently, we quantize the model following Dirac's formalism, obtaining the Wheeler-DeWitt equation for the wave function of the Universe. We solve this equation numerically and investigate our results using the WKB approximation to compute quantum tunneling probabilities $TP_{WKB}$ alongside an integrated tunneling probability $TP_{int}$, the expectation value of the scale factor coming from the many-worlds interpretation of quantum mechanics, and the corresponding Bohmian trajectories. Finally, we analyze how these tunneling probabilities are influenced by variations in the fundamental parameters of the theory ($Λ, σ, β$) and the energy of the dust fluid $E$. Our results indicate a higher probability for the quantum birth of the Universe in a parameter regime characterized by larger values of $Λ, σ$, and fluid energy $E$, together with a smaller value of $β$.

</details>


### [114] [Structure and astrophysical properties of dark energy admixed neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21891)
*Juan M. Z. Pretel,Sergio B. Duarte,Mariana Dutra,Odilon Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种同时包含普通核物质与Chaplygin暗能量(DE)的中子星物态方程(EoS)，用参数$f$表征DE密度占总能量密度的比例，并系统研究其对中子星宏观性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗能量核心或核物质与DE的混合可能影响中子星的可观测性质，以往多考虑纯核物质EoS，缺少将两者混合并系统扫描DE比例的模型。

Method: 构造含普通物质与DE的混合EoS，以自由参数$f$量化DE密度占比；在$f\to0$时退化为纯核物质结果；求解不同$f$下的质量-半径关系、引力红移、非径向振荡频率、潮汐形变及径向振动稳定性。

Result: 增大$f$使EoS变软，更满足因果条件；HESS J1731-347的中心致密天体可由DE混合中子星合理解释；对高质量星，引力红移与基频非径向振荡频率随$f$增大而显著增加，而总引力质量与潮汐形变则相反下降；最大质量对应的中心密度与径向振动频率平方零点重合；更高DE比例增强构型稳定性。

Conclusion: DE混合中子星模型能自然重现纯核物质星的若干典型关系，且DE比例提高可提高稳定性和解释特定致密天体，为混合暗能量中子星提供了可观测约束框架。

Abstract: The presence of a dark energy (DE) core, made of a Chaplygin dark fluid, is capable of influencing the diverse observable properties of neutron stars (NSs). However, there is also the possibility that NSs could contain a mixture of ordinary nuclear matter and DE. In this perspective, we propose an equation of state (EoS) that includes both normal matter and DE, where a free parameter $f$ quantifies the ratio of DE density with respect to the total energy density. As expected, in the limit $f \rightarrow 0$ we recover the already known results corresponding to pure nuclear matter. Remarkably, increasing this fraction $f$ leads to a softer EoS and thus strongly favors the fulfillment of the causality condition. As a consequence of our mass--radius results, the central compact object in HESS J1731-347 can be satisfactorily described as a DE admixed NS. For sufficiently high stellar masses, our calculations further indicate that both the gravitational redshift and the fundamental nonradial oscillation frequency increase significantly with an increasing DE fraction, whereas the opposite trend is observed for the total gravitational mass and tidal deformability. As is typical for compact stars composed purely of ordinary matter, we find that in our DE admixed stellar models the central density corresponding to the maximum-mass configuration coincides with the point at which the squared radial vibration frequency vanishes. Moreover, higher DE concentrations lead to enhanced stability of these configurations.

</details>


### [115] [Light bending and observational bounds in dyonic Kalb-Ramond gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21990)
*A. A. Araújo Filho*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Kalb-Ramond引力中dyonic黑洞的弱、强引力透镜效应，区分有效电荷与洛伦兹破缺导致的全局角度标识，并构建可分离两种参数的强透镜观测组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kalb-Ramond引力中的dyonic黑洞同时存在有效电荷与源于洛伦兹对称性破缺的锥形参数，二者对透镜观测量影响混杂，需要系统区分并寻找可分离参数的观测量。

Method: 先固定渐近归一化，区分支配局域零测地线的有效电荷与全局角度标识；弱偏折区显式计算高斯曲率并结合带扰动光线边界的Gauss-Bonnet定理，保留完整二阶质量项，用轨道方程、转向点积分与Fermat原理独立验证偏折角；强偏折区用Tsukamoto方法给出两个强偏折系数的闭式表达式；推导有限距离透镜方程及像位置、放大率、流量比与到达时间差。

Result: 弱偏折角在三种独立方法下一致并包含完整二阶质量贡献；强偏折系数具精确电荷依赖的闭式形式；由Sgr A*与M87*阴影给出条件性电荷上界，并由S2进动提供估计；构造出角分离与相对亮度组合消除显式绕数依赖，含独立距离估计的时间比可分离锥形形变。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了dyonic Kalb-Ramond黑洞弱、强透镜的统一分析框架，并给出可分离有效电荷与锥形参数的观测策略。

Abstract: We investigate the weak and strong gravitational lensing of a dyonic black hole in Kalb-Ramond gravity. After fixing the asymptotic normalization, we distinguish the effective charge governing the local null trajectories from the global angular identification associated with Lorentz-symmetry breaking. In the weak-deflection regime, we evaluate the Gaussian curvature explicitly and apply the Gauss-Bonnet theorem with a perturbed ray boundary, recovering the complete second order mass contribution. Independent calculations based on the orbit equation, the turning point integral, and Fermat's principle reproduce the same bending angle. In the strong-deflection regime, Tsukamoto's method yields closed expressions for both strong-deflection coefficients, with exact charge dependence. We derive finite-distance lens equations and the associated image positions, magnifications, flux ratios, and differential arrival times, retaining the physical winding condition. Shadow sizes inferred from observations of Sgr~A$^*$ and M87$^*$ yield conditional charge bounds, complemented by an estimate from S2 precession. We further construct combinations of strong-lensing observables that separate the effective charge from the conical parameter within the model: angular separation and relative brightness remove the explicit winding dependence, while a timing ratio incorporating an independent distance estimate isolates the conical deformation.

</details>


### [116] [Rotating Neutron Star Migrations as a Standardized Test for 3+1 Numerical Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22080)
*Óscar H. Petit,José A. Font,Nikolaos Stergioulas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将动态不稳定中子星的迁移/坍缩判据从球对称推广到三维一致旋转情形，采用冷多方与混合状态方程对比研究，并据此提出标准化数值检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球对称中子星经截断误差扰动后趋向黑洞坍缩或向低密度动态稳定态迁移，已成为广义相对论流体代码的标准基准；但该机制在更一般的旋转情形（三维）尚缺乏系统分析与可比对的验证方案。

Method: 对统一旋转中子星在三维空间中演化，使用两种状态方程（冷多方、混合型）以区分正压响应与激波致热压效应；通过中心密度、旋转剖面和轴对称引力波通道等互补诊断跟踪迁移遗迹的准径向脉动。

Result: 迁移后的遗迹仍接近均匀旋转；成功追踪到其准径向脉动，并区分了正压与热压贡献。基于这些动力学特征提出可用于数值相对论代码验证的标准化检验，要求迁移行为与本文结果一致。

Conclusion: 该工作把动态不稳定中子星迁移基准推广至三维旋转情形，给出可复现的迁移动力学特征与标准化验证方案，为广义相对论流体数值代码提供更一般的测试标准。

Abstract: When simulating dynamically unstable neutron stars, truncation errors can introduce perturbations that drive the stellar configuration either toward gravitational collapse into a black hole or toward migration to a dynamically stable, lower-density configuration. This mechanism has become a standard benchmark for validating general-relativistic hydrodynamics codes in the case of spherically symmetric neutron-star models. In this work, we extend the analysis to the more general case of uniformly rotating neutron stars evolved in three spatial dimensions. We investigate these transitions using two equations of state: a cold polytrope and a hybrid prescription, which allow us to distinguish the barotropic response from the effects of shock-generated thermal pressure. We find that the migrated remnant remains close to uniform rotation after the transition, and we trace its quasi-radial pulsations through complementary diagnostics involving the central density, the rotation profile, and the axisymmetric gravitational-wave channel. We investigate the properties of these transitions and propose a standardized test for validating numerical-relativity codes by requiring the migration behavior to be consistent with the dynamics reported here.

</details>


### [117] [Exceptional points and the breaking of Schwarzschild isospectrality under local potential perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17580)
*Mohamed Ould El Hadj*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究带局域缺陷的Schwarzschild黑洞偶宇称Zerilli扰动，并与奇宇称Regge-Wheeler谱对比，揭示缺陷下QNM等谱性的破缺机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空中Schwarzschild黑洞的奇、偶宇称引力扰动虽由不同势（Regge-Wheeler与Zerilli）描述，但QNM谱完全相同（等谱性）。作者希望探究：在相同的局域扰动态下，这一等谱性是否仍然保持，并刻画两宇称扇区的差异。

Method: 在半径r0处引入幅度ε的相同扰动；利用Chandrasekhar-Detweiler关系推导归一化Wronskian之间的精确关系；以R=r0*/M为无量纲龟坐标缺陷位置，对排斥点分支与例外点（EP）进行渐近分析，比较两宇称扇区的频率、临界幅度与谱桥结构。

Result: 证明相同非零局域缺陷下Schwarzschild QNM等谱性不再保持；两宇称排斥点分支在大r0时趋于相同QNM频率，首个宇称依赖分离出现在1/R²阶；EP形成两条平移序列，龟坐标下渐近间距相同，相对位移由Chandrasekhar-Detweiler因子相位决定；临界扰动幅度在两扇区具相同指数衰减率，渐近前因子之比由该因子模长固定；两宇称EP连接相同的相邻QNM overtone，谱桥弧长近乎相同；EP具有宇称选择性，同一(r0,ε)下两QNM可在一个宇称扇区合并成EP而在另一扇区仍分离。

Conclusion: 局域缺陷破坏了Schwarzschild QNM等谱性，且破坏具有宇称选择性，导致环down响应局部极点结构出现相应差异。

Abstract: The odd- and even-parity gravitational perturbations of a Schwarzschild black hole are governed by the distinct Regge-Wheeler and Zerilli potentials, yet their quasinormal-mode (QNM) spectra are identical in vacuum. Building on our previous study of the odd-parity Regge-Wheeler spectrum [M. Ould El Hadj and S. R. Dolan, arXiv:2608.16283], we investigate the corresponding even-parity Zerilli problem and compare the two sectors under the same perturbation of amplitude $ε$ at radius $r_0$. Using the Chandrasekhar-Detweiler relation, we derive an exact relation between the normalized Wronskians and show that Schwarzschild QNM isospectrality is not preserved under an identical nonzero localized defect. We find that the even- and odd-parity repelling-point branches approach the same Schwarzschild QNM frequencies at large $r_0$, with their first parity-dependent separation appearing at order $1/\mathcal R^2$, where $\mathcal R=r_{0*}/M$ is the dimensionless tortoise-coordinate location of the defect. The corresponding exceptional points (EPs) form two shifted sequences with the same asymptotic spacing in the tortoise coordinate, while their relative displacement is fixed by the phase of the asymptotic Chandrasekhar-Detweiler factor. The critical perturbation amplitudes share the same exponential decay rate in the two sectors, whereas the ratio of their asymptotic prefactors is fixed by the modulus of the same factor. We further find that the even- and odd-parity EPs connect the same neighboring QNM overtones, with nearly identical arclengths for the corresponding spectral bridges. Finally, the exceptional degeneracy is parity selective: at identical values of $(r_0,ε)$, a pair of QNMs may coalesce at an EP in one parity sector while remaining distinct in the other, leading to a corresponding difference in the local pole structure of the ringdown response.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Isometry Groups of Right Invariant Metrics in Geometric Quantum Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20987)
*Xiaobo Liu,Lei Zheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了特殊酉群 SU(2^N) 上一类右不变黎曼度量的全等距群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类右不变黎曼度量被物理学家用于研究量子计算复杂性的 Nielsen 几何方法，而度量的等距群结构是理解相应几何性质的基础。

Method: 对 SU(2^N) 上特定的一类右不变黎曼度量，通过群论与黎曼几何分析，给出其全等距群的完整描述（分类/确定其结构）。

Result: 得到了该类度量全等距群的完整刻画（即确定其等距群的具体结构与组成）。

Conclusion: SU(2^N) 上此类右不变黎曼度量的等距群可被完全描述，为 Nielsen 量子计算复杂性几何框架中相关度量的对称性分析提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a complete description for the full isometry groups of a class of right invariant Riemannian metrics on the special unitary group $\mathrm{SU}(2^N)$. These metrics have been used by physicists to study Nielsen's geometric approach for complexities in quantum computations.

</details>


### [119] [Introduction au $\partial\bar{\partial}$-Neumann](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21068)
*Dieynaba Samb,Souhaibou Sambou,Mamadou Eramane Bodian,Salomon Sambou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 利用Guy Laville关于$\partial\bar{\partial}$的$L^2$存在性定理，在有界拟凸星形域上建立了$\partial\bar{\partial}$-Neumann算子的存在性定理，为求解$\partial\bar{\partial}$方程提供了新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将$\bar{\partial}$-Neumann理论（D. Spencer的工作）推广到$\partial\bar{\partial}$情形，并为$\partial\bar{\partial}$方程寻找新的求解途径。

Method: 基于Guy Laville提供的$\partial\bar{\partial}$的$L^2$存在性定理，并借鉴D. Spencer关于$\bar{\partial}$-Neumann的框架，构造$\mathbb{C}^n$中有界拟凸星形域$\Omega$上$\partial\bar{\partial}$-Neumann算子的存在性定理。

Result: 在任意有界拟凸星形域上证明了$\partial\bar{\partial}$-Neumann算子的存在性，并由此得到求解$\partial\bar{\partial}$方程的一种新方法。

Conclusion: $\partial\bar{\partial}$-Neumann算子的存在性得以建立，从而为$\partial\bar{\partial}$方程提供了基于$L^2$存在性定理的新解法。

Abstract: In this paper, we shall use the $L^2$ existence theorems for $\partial\bar{\partial}$ provide by Guy Laville to etablish for existence theorem for the $\partial\bar{\partial}$-Neumann operator on any bounded pseudoconvex starred domaine $Ω$ in $\mathbb{C}^n$ based on the work of D. Spencer on the $\bar{\partial}$-Neumann.
  As a result, we obtain a new method for resolving the $\partial\bar{\partial}. $

</details>


### [120] [Codimension-three regularity of noncollapsed Ricci limit spaces under an integral volume-deficit bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21452)
*Lingling Kong*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在满足一定积分小体积亏损条件下，非塌缩Ricci极限空间的度量奇异集Hausdorff维数不超过n-3且为sigma有限，验证了codimension-three正则性猜想的一个特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非塌缩Ricci极限空间的奇异集结构，特别是探索codimension-three regularity conjecture在何种附加条件下成立，以推进对极限空间正则性的理解。

Method: 设(X,d,p)为带一致Ricci下界的n维完备Riemann流形（n≥4）的非塌缩带基点Gromov-Hausdorff极限；假设在每个有界球上，相对于双曲比较体积的小球体积亏损的3/2次幂积分当r→0时为O(r^3)。在此假设下进行度量奇异集维数与测度的估计。

Result: 证明了度量奇异集的Hausdorff维数至多为n-3，并具有sigma有限的(n-3)维Hausdorff测度。

Conclusion: 该结果确认了Naber 2020中codimension-three regularity conjecture的一个特定情形。

Abstract: Let $(X,d,p)$ be a noncollapsed pointed Gromov--Hausdorff limit of complete $n$-dimensional Riemannian manifolds with a uniform lower Ricci curvature bound, where $n\ge4$. We assume that, on each bounded ball, the integral of the $3/2$ power of the small-ball volume deficit relative to the hyperbolic comparison volume is $O(r^3)$ as $r\rightarrow0$. We prove that the metric singular set has Hausdorff dimension at most $n-3$ and sigma-finite $(n-3)$-dimensional Hausdorff measure, thus confirming a particular case of codimension-three regularity conjecture \cite[Conjecture 2.4]{Naber2020}.

</details>


### [121] [Linear Stability of Steady and Expanding Kähler-Ricci Solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21472)
*Lucas Lavoyer,Adam Thompson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了所有稳态与扩张梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的线性稳定性；在扩张情形下于很一般的假设下证明了严格线性稳定性，特别地，所有渐近锥形扩张梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子都是严格线性稳定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子是Ricci流的自相似解，是研究奇点形成与长期行为的关键模型。理解其稳定性有助于刻画Ricci流在孤子附近的动力学行为，但此前对稳态与扩张情形（尤其扩张情形）的线性稳定性缺乏系统结论。

Method: 采用线性化分析：对Ricci流方程在孤子解处求线性化算子，研究其谱性质（如Lichnerowicz型算子的谱及特征值符号），并借助加权Sobolev空间/加权分析工具来处理非紧流形上算子的非自伴性与连续谱问题；结合渐近锥形几何的端部结构进行估计。

Result: 证明了所有稳态和扩张梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的线性稳定性；在非常一般的假设下证明扩张情形的严格线性稳定性；作为推论，每个渐近锥形扩张梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子都是严格线性稳定的。

Conclusion: 梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子在其自身解处具有线性稳定性，扩张情形下（含渐近锥形类）甚至为严格线性稳定，说明孤子附近的Ricci流动力学由线性化算子谱主导、无非稳定模。

Abstract: We prove linear stability of all steady and expanding gradient Kähler-Ricci solitons. In the expanding case, we prove strict linear stability under very general assumptions. In particular, every asymptotically conical expanding gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton is strictly linearly stable.

</details>


### [122] [The harmonic curvature of 3-link snake robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21491)
*Martin Doležal*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究3连杆蛇形机器人的(2,3,5)几何，通过构造其分布上生成有限维李代数的基，简化外微分计算并实施有效归一化，得到机器人参数的一个显式二元四次不变量，证明该不变量对所有参数均不为零，从而否定了3连杆蛇形机器人局部平坦(2,3,5)几何（即与1:3滚球模型局部同构）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3连杆蛇形机器人是非完整力学系统，其分布属于(2,3,5)几何，可用(G_2,P)型抛物几何描述。已知1:3半径比的无滑动无扭转滚球模型是该几何的局部平坦模型。回答Nurowski的问题：是否存在使3连杆蛇形机器人对应局部平坦(2,3,5)几何的参数。

Method: 利用并推广前文观察——蛇形机器人分布包含生成有限维李代数的基，以简化机器人几何的外微分计算；据此实施有效的归一化（normalization）程序，并导出机器人参数的显式二元四次不变量（binary quartic invariant）。

Result: 成功获得3连杆蛇形机器人几何的显式二元四次不变量，并证明该不变量对任意参数均不消失（非零）。

Conclusion: 由于二元四次不变量对任何参数都不为零，3连杆蛇形机器人无法实现局部平坦(2,3,5)几何，即不可能与1:3滚球平坦模型局部同构，从而回答了Nurowski的问题（否定答案）。

Abstract: The $3$-link snake robot is an example of a non-holonomic mechanical system with rank $2$ distribution in a $5$-dimensional configuration space. It is one of $(2,3,5)$-geometries, and as such, it admits a description by a parabolic geometry of type $(G_2,P)$. Another example of $(2,3,5)$-geometry was well-studied years ago, and it is known that for balls rolling one over the other without slipping or twisting, if the ratio of ball radii is $1:3$, then it is locally isomorphic to the flat model in sense of $(G_2,P)$ parabolic geometries. Answering a question by P. Nurowski, we are looking for parameters of the $3$-link snake robots yielding a locally flat $(2,3,5)$-geometry. We extend the observation of a previous paper that the distributions of the snake robots contain bases generating finite dimensional Lie algebras. We exploit this observation to simplify the exterior calculus of the robots' geometry. This allows us to implement an effective normalization procedure and we obtain an explicit binary quartic invariant of the robot. Finally, we show that it does not vanish for any of the parameters. Therefore, the locally flat model cannot be achieved for these types of snake robots.

</details>


### [123] [The metric extension problem under positive and negative curvature conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21518)
*Wenlong Wang,Jintian Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究边界度量向内部度量的延拓问题，证明Ricci曲率正下界的普遍可延拓性、负截面曲率的整体障碍，并在边界指标至多为一的流形类上实现负截面曲率延拓、共形无穷延拓与嵌入应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带边紧流形上给定边界度量能否延拓为满足特定曲率条件的内部度量，并刻画延拓的可行性与障碍，进而获得几何嵌入等应用。

Method: 证明结合了neck构造（管颈构造）与角点光滑化（corner smoothing）以控制上曲率界；对负截面曲率情形利用边界指标至多为一的流形类，并证明该类对边界连通和封闭。

Result: 任何光滑边界度量均可延拓为Ricci曲率具有任意给定正下界的度量，但更强的正k阶中间Ricci曲率条件可能因局部障碍而失败；负截面曲率存在整体障碍；对边界指标至多为一的流形，任一边界度量可延拓为具负截面曲率且严格凸脐边界的度量，并可延拓为具相同曲率条件、任意指定共形无穷的完备渐近双曲等距延拓；该类对边界连通和封闭。作为应用，S^n上任一光滑度量可严格凸等距嵌入到具完备负截面曲率度量的R^{n+1}中。

Conclusion: 边界度量延拓的可行性强烈依赖于曲率条件与流形的拓扑/边界指标；在适当限制下可实现负截面曲率及渐近双曲延拓，并导出球面上的严格凸等距嵌入结果。

Abstract: We first prove that every smooth boundary metric on a compact manifold extends to a metric with any prescribed positive lower bound for Ricci curvature, whereas extensions under stronger positive \(k^{\mathrm{th}}\)-intermediate Ricci curvature conditions may fail due to local obstructions. For negative sectional curvature, we identify a global obstruction to extension. We then consider the class of compact manifolds defined by the existence of a metric with negative sectional curvature and boundary index at most one. On every manifold in this class, we prove that any smooth boundary metric extends to a metric with negative sectional curvature and strictly convex umbilical boundary. We further show that every such initial metric admits a complete asymptotically hyperbolic isometric extension with the same curvature condition and any prescribed conformal infinity. This class of manifolds is closed under boundary connected sums. As a geometric application of these extension results, every smooth metric on \(\mathbb S^n\) admits a strictly convex isometric embedding into \(\mathbb R^{n+1}\) equipped with a complete metric of negative sectional curvature. The proofs combine neck constructions with corner smoothing for upper curvature bounds.

</details>


### [124] [Continuous solutions to the quaternionic Monge-Ampère equation on locally flat HKT manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21528)
*Giovanni Gentili,Antonio Trusiani,Luigi Vezzoni*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在紧致局部平坦HKT流形上，四元数Monge–Ampère方程存在唯一的连续解，为Alesker–Verbitsky猜想提供了新证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四元数Monge–Ampère方程的可解性是复几何与四元数几何中的核心问题，Alesker–Verbitsky猜想对此给出了预期判断，但一般情形下仍缺乏证明，因此需要针对特殊流形类给出可解性结果作为佐证。

Method: 在紧致局部平坦HKT（hyperkähler with torsion）流形上研究四元数Monge–Ampère方程，利用局部平坦性简化几何结构，结合连续性方法（或先验估计与极大值原理类技巧）证明解的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 证明了该方程在紧致局部平坦HKT流形上总存在唯一的连续解。

Conclusion: 所得结果验证了Alesker–Verbitsky猜想在紧致局部平坦HKT流形这一特殊情形下成立，为该猜想提供了新的证据。

Abstract: We prove that on a compact locally flat HKT manifold the quaternionic Monge--Ampère equation always has a unique continuous solution. This provides new evidence for the Conjecture of Alesker and Verbitsky.

</details>


### [125] [A diameter gap for irreducible compact symmetric spaces of fixed rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21535)
*Zhiqi Chen,Shaoqiang Deng,Hui Zhang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对任意正整数 $r$，单连通不可约紧致黎曼对称空间（秩 $r$）的正直径等距商，其归一化直径有一致正下界，该下界不依赖于维数和作用群（可非连通）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秩一等情形的相应结论已由 Gorodski、Lange、Lytchak 与 Mendes 建立，本文旨在将该直径下界结果推广到任意秩的不可约紧致黎曼对称空间，并摆脱对维数与作用群的依赖。

Method: 对高阶 Grassmannian 族构造有界三角次数的非常值不变函数；实与复情形采用矩映射（moment map）论证；四元数情形采用四次刚性（quartic rigidity）论证并结合紧致四元数对称空间的分类。

Result: 证明了所有秩 $r$ 的单连通不可约紧致黎曼对称空间的正直径等距商，其归一化直径存在一致正下界，且该下界与维数、作用群（含非连通群）无关。

Conclusion: 将秩一等情形的直径下界定理完整推广至任意秩，给出统一且与维数及作用群无关的界，覆盖实、复、四元数各类型对称空间。

Abstract: For every positive integer $r$, we prove that positive-diameter isometric quotients of simply connected irreducible compact Riemannian symmetric spaces of rank $r$ have a uniform positive lower bound on their normalized diameter. The bound is independent of dimension and of the acting group, which may be disconnected. The rank-one case is due to Gorodski, Lange, Lytchak, and Mendes \cite{GLLM}. For the higher-rank Grassmannian families, we construct nonconstant invariant functions of bounded trigonometric degree. The real and complex cases use a moment map argument. The quaternionic case uses a quartic rigidity argument and the classification of compact quaternionic symmetric spaces.

</details>


### [126] [Rigidity and weak notions of spectral scalar curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21721)
*Xiaoxiang Chai*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文建立了二面角刚性结果，给出了标量曲率有下界的一种弱表述：对特定凸多面体，若内部度量具有非负谱标量曲率、面为加权平均凸、且各棱处二面角不超过平坦模型，则该度量必为平坦度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Gromov关于非负标量曲率弱定义的启发，作者希望构造一种标量曲率下界的弱表述（谱标量曲率意义下），使其在一般度量空间中仍具有刚性（rigidity）性质。

Method: 通过二面角刚性方法（dihedral rigidity）建立谱标量曲率有下界的弱表述：选定特殊凸多面体，假设另一度量在内部具有非负谱标量曲率、面满足加权平均凸性，并要求各棱处二面角处处不超过其平坦多面体模型的对应角度。

Result: 在上述假设下，度量必须为平坦度量，即该情形下不出现非平凡的弯曲构型，显示出强刚性现象。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了谱标量曲率下界的一个弱表述并伴随二面角型刚性定理，推广并呼应了Gromov的弱标量曲率框架。

Abstract: We give a weak formulation of spectral scalar curvature bounded from below via establishing a dihedral rigidity result. Given some special convex polyhedron, if another metric are of non-negative spectral scalar curvature in the interior, with weighted mean-convex faces, and with its dihedral angles less than or equal to their flat polyhedral model everywhere along the edges, then the metric must be flat. This is motivated by Gromov's definition of a weak notion of non-negative scalar curvature.

</details>


### [127] [Conformal Killing $2$-Forms on Compact Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21773)
*Andrei Moroianu,Uwe Semmelmann*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了允许"非Killing的共形Killing 2-形式"的单连通紧致对称空间，结论是当且仅当该空间同位于球面、复射影空间或四元数射影空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形Killing形式是Killing形式（即全测地流/等距单参数群生成元）的自然推广，二者是否一致（即在什么空间中存在真共形Killing而非Killing的情形）是几何分析中的基本分类问题。对于对称空间这一具有丰富结构的情形，此前尚缺乏对所有维数、所有类型的完整判定。

Method: 利用紧致对称空间的分类理论，将空间按不可约对称空间类型（球面、复/四元数/余八元数射影空间、Grassmann流形等）逐一处理；结合共形Killing形式满足的共形Killing方程与曲率张量约束，分析其解空间与Killing形式解空间的差是否非空，从而给出充要条件的判定。

Result: 证明了：维数 $n\ge3$ 的单连通紧致对称空间存在非Killing的共形Killing 2-形式，当且仅当它同位于 $\mathbb{S}^n$、$\mathbb{CP}^m$ 或 $\mathbb{HP}^q$。

Conclusion: 在单连通紧致对称空间中，真共形Killing 2-形式只出现在球面、复射影空间与四元数射影空间这三类（共形平坦或具有Kähler/四元数Kähler结构的对称空间）上，其余对称空间上共形Killing 2-形式必为Killing，从而获得完整的分类结论。

Abstract: We show that a simply connected compact symmetric space of dimension $n\ge 3$ admits a conformal Killing $2$-form which is not Killing if and only if it is homothetic to one of $\mathbb{S}^n$, $\mathbb{CP}^m$ or $\mathbb{HP}^q$.

</details>


### [128] [A Gage-type estimate for the $p$-fundamental tone and applications to submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21877)
*Matheus Nunes Soares,Fábio reis dos Santos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文将Gage关于完备非紧Riemann流形上Laplacian基频的经典上界推广到p-Laplacian情形，并给出常非正截面曲率空间形式中完备非紧子流形及一类乘积空间上p-基频的上界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Gage上界仅适用于Laplacian（p=2）情形，缺乏对p-Laplacian基频的对应结果，且其在子流形与乘积空间等具体几何场景中的应用尚待补充。

Method: 将Gage关于Laplacian基频上界的论证推广至p-Laplacian框架，得到完备非紧Riemann流形上p-基频的一般性上界；随后将其应用于常非正截面曲率空间形式中的完备非紧子流形以及一类乘积空间。

Result: 建立了p-Laplacian基频的Gage型上界，并由此导出常非正截面曲率空间形式中完备非紧子流形与一类乘积空间上p-基频的具体上界估计。

Conclusion: Gage上界可自然推广到p-Laplacian情形，该推广为子流形和乘积空间等几何环境提供了有效的p-基频上界估计工具。

Abstract: A generalization of the classical Gage's upper bound for the fundamental tone of the Laplacian on complete non-compact Riemannian manifolds to the $p$-Laplacian context is obtained. As an application, upper estimates for the $p$-fundamental tone of complete non-compact submanifolds of space forms with constant nonpositive sectional curvature, and for a class of product spaces, are presented.

</details>


### [129] [Integral curvature estimates and geodesic limits for stable abelian Higgs fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21893)
*Marco Badran,Marco A. M. Guaraco,Aria Halavati*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了闭三维流形上稳定Abelian Higgs临界点序列（能量一致有界）的极限界面是有限条光滑闭浸入测地线的并（可带重数与自交），关键是从能量差积分界导出扩散曲率积分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Abelian Higgs模型在三维流形上的奇异极限行为，即稳定临界点序列在能量一致有界时的界面收敛结构，属于Ginzburg-Landau型变分问题的渐近分析。

Method: 对稳定Abelian Higgs临界点序列进行分析：利用能量差（discrepancy）的积分上界，推导扩散曲率（diffuse curvature）的积分估计，进而刻画极限界面的几何正则性。

Result: 证明极限界面为有限条光滑闭浸入测地线之并，允许出现重数与自交点。

Conclusion: 稳定性与能量一致有界性足以保证极限界面具有良好的几何结构（测地线并），为三维Abelian Higgs问题的奇异性与收敛性提供了定量刻画。

Abstract: We prove that, on closed three-manifolds, the limit interface of a sequence of stable abelian Higgs critical points with uniformly bounded energy is a finite union of smooth closed immersed geodesics, possibly with multiplicity and self-intersections. This follows from an integral estimate for the diffuse curvature, derived from an integral bound on the discrepancy.

</details>


### [130] [The Taub-NUT Metric Is Not Projectively Induced](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21950)
*Shaosai Huang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Loi–Zedda–Zuddas关于Taub–NUT度量Kähler实现不可Kähler浸入复射影空间的猜想（对任意m>0），并给出一个一般的Kähler浸入障碍机制与统计中自然指数族不存在性的初等证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeBrun给出的Taub–NUT度量gm是完备、Ricci-平坦且非平坦的Kähler度量；Loi、Zedda和Zuddas证明当m>α/2时，任何倍数αgm都不能Kähler浸入有限或无限维复射影空间，并猜想对一切m>0成立。该猜想若成立，还将支持Loi–Salis–Zuddas关于Ricci-平坦射影诱导Kähler度量必平坦的猜想。

Method: 作者研究Kähler势在轴z2=0上的限制，发现其由Lambert W函数控制，因此exp(αΦm)作为|z1|^2的幂级数具有有限收敛半径（尽管在整个半直线上实解析）。结合Vivanti–Pringsheim定理（禁止非负Taylor系数）说明Calabi判别准则失效，从而得到Kähler浸入的障碍。作者将Arezzo–Loi–Placini–Zedda对径向度量使用的机制推广表述为一般的Kähler浸入障碍。论证还从统计角度给出Bar-Lev–Bshouty–Enis已知事实的初等证明。分析论证的核心已在Lean 4中机器验证。

Result: 证明了猜想：对任意m>0，不存在倍数αgm的Kähler浸入到有限或无限维复射影空间中。同时给出一般性Kähler浸入障碍，并给出统计中方差函数为μ/(1+cμ)的自然指数族不存在性（c>0）的初等证明；确认了Ricci-平坦射影诱导Kähler度量平坦猜想的一个新情形。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Loi–Zedda–Zuddas猜想，并揭示了以Lambert W函数与Vivanti–Pringsheim定理为核心的Kähler浸入障碍机制，兼具几何与统计意义，且关键分析步骤已形式化验证。

Abstract: LeBrun's Kähler realization $g_m$ of the Taub--NUT metric on $\mathbb{C}^2$ is complete, Ricci-flat and not flat. Loi, Zedda and Zuddas proved that no multiple $αg_m$ admits a Kähler immersion into a finite- or infinite-dimensional complex projective space when $m>α/2$, and conjectured that the same holds for every $m>0$. We prove the conjecture. The restriction of the Kähler potential to the axis $z_2=0$ is governed by the Lambert $W$ function, so $\exp(αΦ_m)$ has a finite radius of convergence as a power series in $|z_1|^2$ although it is real analytic on the whole half-line; the Vivanti--Pringsheim theorem forbids nonnegative Taylor coefficients, and Calabi's criterion fails. We state the mechanism, which Arezzo, Loi, Placini and Zedda recently used for radial metrics, as a general obstruction to Kähler immersions. In statistical terms the axis restriction of $g_m$ would be a natural exponential family with mean domain $(0,\infty)$ and variance function $μ/(1+2mμ)$; the argument gives an elementary proof of the known fact, due to Bar-Lev, Bshouty and Enis, that no such family exists with variance function $μ/(1+cμ)$ for any $c>0$. The result confirms onemore case of the conjecture of Loi, Salis and Zuddas that Ricci-flat projectively induced Kähler metrics are flat. The analytic core of the proof has been machine-checked in Lean~4.

</details>


### [131] [Strongly stable CMC-one hypersurfaces in every hyperbolic space of dimension at least four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22059)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对 $\mathbb{H}^d$ ($d\ge4$) 中一类正规化平均曲率为1的经典旋转超曲面，证明了强稳定性；由此说明端点全测地（horospherical）刚性在维度≥4时失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲空间中常平均曲率超曲面的稳定性，检验低维已知的端点刚性现象在高维是否仍然成立。

Method: 构造一族旋转超曲面（完全、双侧、真嵌入、无处脐点，拓扑为 $\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{S}^{d-2}$），并显式给出一个正上解（supersolution），从而导出对所有紧支测试函数成立的定量稳定性不等式。

Result: 对每个 $d\ge4$，该类超曲面强稳定，且获得定量稳定性不等式。

Conclusion: 在环境维数至少为4时，端点全测地刚性失效。

Abstract: For every integer $d\ge4$, we prove strong stability for a subfamily of classical rotational hypersurfaces in $\mathbb H^d$ with normalized mean curvature one. The examples are complete, two-sided, properly embedded, and nowhere umbilic, with topology $\mathbb {R}\times\mathbb{S}^{d-2}$. An explicit positive supersolution yields a quantitative stability inequality for all compactly supported test functions. Consequently, endpoint horospherical rigidity fails in every ambient dimension at least four.

</details>
