### [132] [Calculating Axion-Matter Couplings in String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20911)
*Joshua N. Benabou,Naomi Gendler,Thomas R. Harvey,Jakob Moritz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文首次在弦理论中显式计算轴子-物质耦合的主导微扰贡献，对象为杂化弦理论的Calabi-Yau紧化（向量丛为线丛之和），利用神经网络求解涉及Ricci平坦度量、Hermitian Yang-Mills丛度量和物质场调和形式的耦合微分方程，揭示了四维耦合的特征层级：模型依赖轴子对费米子耦合为O(1)，模型无关轴子耦合被α_YM压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子-物质耦合在轴子唯象学中至关重要，但此前缺乏从弦理论出发对微扰贡献的显式计算；本文旨在填补这一空白，给出弦理论中轴子-物质耦合的第一性原理结果。

Method: 在杂化弦理论的Calabi-Yau紧化中（向量丛为线丛之和）计算轴子-物质耦合，将其表示为重叠积分，需要Ricci平坦度量、Hermitian Yang-Mills丛度量以及物质场调和形式；通过神经网络求解相应的耦合微分方程组得到这些量。

Result: 得到四维耦合的特征层级：轴子与费米子耦合形式为L ⊃ c_ψ (∂_μa/f_a) ψ† σ̄^μψ；对于模型依赖轴子，c_ψ是Kähler模的O(1)函数；而模型无关轴子的相应耦合在紫外匹配标度处被α_YM压低。模型依赖轴子的费米子耦合类似DFSZ型四维轴子，模型无关轴子的耦合则如KSVZ型构造那样受到圈图压低。

Conclusion: 所发展的方法可直接应用于具有现实谱的大量杂化弦线丛模型，并可推广到II型弦构造。

Abstract: In this work we calculate, for the first time, the dominant perturbative contributions to axion--matter couplings explicitly in string theory. Concretely, we compute these couplings in Calabi--Yau compactifications of heterotic string theory where the vector bundle is a sum of line bundles. They are expressed as overlap integrals requiring the Ricci-flat metric, the Hermitian Yang-Mills bundle metric, and the matter field harmonic forms. We compute these by solving the corresponding coupled differential equations with neural networks. Our results establish a characteristic hierarchy of four-dimensional couplings: axions couple to fermions as $\mathcal{L} \supset c_ψ\,\frac{\partial_μa}{f_a}\, ψ^\dagger \barσ^μψ$ with $c_ψ$ an $\mathcal{O}(1)$ function of Kähler moduli for the model-dependent axions, while the corresponding coupling for the model-independent axion is suppressed by $α_{\rm YM}$ at the ultraviolet matching scale. The model-dependent axions thus couple to fermions similarly as DFSZ-like four-dimensional axions, whereas the model-independent axion's couplings are loop-suppressed as in KSVZ-like constructions. The methods developed here apply directly to the large class of heterotic line bundle models with realistic spectra, and can be adapted to type II constructions.

</details>


### [133] [A Type IIB Dualifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20915)
*Zihni Kaan Baykara,Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证十维IIB弦理论在强耦合不动点τ=exp(2πi/3)处经Z₃对偶对称性轨道折叠后，可定义为一个自洽的非超对称手征十维理论，具有离散规范对称性，其玻色子商为Narain格Γ^{2,2}(A₂)的自同构群，并通过拓扑Green-Schwarz机制消除规范反常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受F理论对偶描述、以及该理论与九维M理论、八维IIA弦理论对偶关系的启发，作者希望构造并论证一个自洽的十维非超对称手征理论，探索其离散规范对称性与反常消除机制。

Method: 通过将十维IIB弦理论在强耦合不动点τ=exp(2πi/3)处对Z₃对偶对称性进行轨道折叠；利用其F理论对偶以及同九维M理论、八维IIA理论的对偶性进行分析；识别十维快子场与Narain模量的对应关系，并借助Z₃值的两形式规范对称性的拓扑Green-Schwarz机制处理规范反常。

Result: 得到的理论为十维手征非超对称理论，具有离散规范对称性，其玻色子商为Narain格Γ^{2,2}(A₂)的自同构群S₃×S₃×Z₂；十维快子场与相应的Narain模量对应；规范反常通过拓扑Green-Schwarz机制非平凡地消除。

Conclusion: 该Z₃轨道折叠的十维IIB理论是自洽的非超对称手征理论，其对称性结构与Narain格自同构群一致，反常消除依赖于Z₃值两形式规范对称性，后者是八维IIA描述中零形式轨道折叠量子对称性的十维提升。

Abstract: We present evidence that ten-dimensional type IIB string theory orbifolded by the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ duality symmetry at the strong-coupling fixed point $τ=\exp(2πi/3)$ defines a consistent theory. Motivated by its F-theory dual description and its duality with M-theory in nine dimensions and type IIA string theory in eight dimensions, we argue that the resulting theory is a ten-dimensional chiral non-supersymmetric theory with a discrete gauge symmetry whose bosonic quotient is the automorphism group of the Narain lattice $Γ^{2,2}(A_2)$ ($S_3 \times S_3 \times \mathbb{Z}_2$). We further identify the ten-dimensional tachyon fields with the corresponding Narain moduli. Gauge anomalies cancel nontrivially through a topological Green-Schwarz mechanism involving a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-valued two-form gauge symmetry: the ten-dimensional lift of the familiar zero-form orbifold quantum symmetry in the eight-dimensional type IIA description.

</details>


### [134] [Discrete Gauging of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ $E_n$ SCFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20917)
*Guillermo Arias-Tamargo,Mario De Marco,Craig Lawrie,Shani Nadir Meynet,Alessandro Mininno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 研究5d秩一E_n SCFT离散对称性的规范化，利用广义环面多边形与(p,q)-膜网识别可扩展至完整SCFT的模空间对称性，构造规范化理论的Higgs分支商作为花环磁振子箭图的Coulomb分支并计算Hilbert级数，提出通量依赖符号的修正单极公式，并猜想更高秩的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解5d秩一E_n SCFT中固定Coulomb分支坐标但在Higgs分支手征环上非平凡作用的离散对称性，以及这些对称性规范化后的模空间结构。

Method: 使用广义环面多边形和(p,q)-膜网识别模空间对称性；将Higgs分支商实现为花环磁振子箭图的Coulomb分支并计算Hilbert级数；将离散作用提升到Seiberg-Witten几何；针对交换相邻规范节点的Z_2花环情形提出通量依赖符号的修正单极公式。

Result: 识别出许多情形下对称性为非反常的；Hilbert级数给出的Coulomb分支商描述成立；从矩映射提取的flavor代数与e_n的不动点子代数一致；Seiberg-Witten几何恢复了对质量变形的预期限制；发现Z_2花环需要通量依赖符号并给出修正方案。

Conclusion: 离散对称性可被规范化并导致结构明确的Higgs分支商，其可由花环磁振子箭图描述；修正后的单极公式处理了相邻节点交换的符号问题；猜想的高秩推广在秩二E_6/Z_2 Higgs分支上得到验证。

Abstract: We study the gauging of discrete symmetries of the 5d rank-one $E_n$ SCFTs that fix the Coulomb branch coordinate but act nontrivially on the Higgs branch chiral ring. Using generalized toric polygons and $(p,q)$-brane webs, we identify symmetries of the moduli space that extend to symmetries of the full SCFT. In many cases they are non-anomalous, and we analyze the moduli space of the gauged theories. We realize the resulting Higgs branch quotients as Coulomb branches of wreathed magnetic quivers and compute their Hilbert series; the flavor algebras extracted from moment maps agree with the fixed-point subalgebras of $\mathfrak{e}_n$. We also lift the discrete actions to the Seiberg--Witten geometry and recover the expected restriction on the mass deformations. For $\mathbb{Z}_2$-wreathings that exchange adjacent gauge nodes, we find that the monopole formula requires a flux-dependent sign and propose a corrected prescription. Finally, we conjecture a higher-rank extension and verify it for the rank-two $E_6/\mathbb{Z}_2$ Higgs branch.

</details>


### [135] [Massive type 0A string theory and M-theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20919)
*Niccolò Cribiori,Andriana Makridou,Salvatore Raucci*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出用M理论"量子纤维化"构造给出0A型弦论两个质量形变的M理论起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0A型弦论的两个大质量形变此前缺乏统一的M理论起源解释，需要在Hull关于大质量IIA的实现以及近期无质量0A的M理论提案基础上进行扩展。

Method: 在M理论中引入称为"量子纤维化"的构造，其中两个独立的整单值性产生Roman质量，并与量子纤维化的两个Chern数T对偶；关键是将0B型对偶群表述为带标记椭圆曲线。

Result: 两个独立整单值性与Roman质量对应，其T对偶对应量子纤维化的两个Chern数；同时讨论了膜与Chern-Simons项的M理论起源及相关疑难。

Conclusion: 该量子纤维化框架为0A型弦论质量形变提供M理论起源，并给出对偶群的新几何表述，但仍遗留膜与Chern-Simons项起源的若干未解问题。

Abstract: We propose an M-theory origin for the two massive deformations of type 0A string theory by extending Hull's realization of massive type IIA in the context of a recent proposal for massless type 0A from M-theory. We introduce an M-theory setup that we call quantum fibration, in which two independent integral monodromies yield the Romans masses, T-dual to the two Chern numbers of the quantum fibration. A key ingredient is a formulation of the type 0B duality group in terms of a marked elliptic curve. We discuss further implications of this description and some puzzles concerning the M-theory origin of branes and Chern--Simons terms.

</details>


### [136] [The doubly conformal dispersion relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20924)
*Maddalena Ferragatta,Balt C. van Rees*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文简化推导了Carmi与Caron-Huot提出的共形色散关系，揭示"魔法变量"x的隐藏共形对称性来源，并引入正规化子证明单次减除即可保证所有积分收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原共形色散关系（由双不连续性重构CFT四点函数）的推导较为复杂，且"魔法变量"x的出现缺乏直观解释，同时积分收敛性问题尚未得到系统处理。

Method: 给出共形色散关系的简化推导；揭示隐藏的共形对称性，将x解释为由四个物理交比构成的简单交比；在双不连续性操作中引入正规化子，并证明单次减除足以使所有积分收敛。

Result: 成功简化了共形色散关系的推导，阐明了x作为物理交比组合的对称性起源，并证明了单次减除的收敛充分性。

Conclusion: 本文为共形色散关系提供了更透明、更简洁的理解框架，明确了魔法变量的对称性本质，并解决了积分收敛的技术问题。

Abstract: We provide a simple derivation of the conformal dispersion relation of Carmi and Caron-Huot which reconstructs a CFT four-point function from its double discontinuity. We exhibit a hidden conformal symmetry which explains the appearance of the "magic variable" x as a simple cross-ratio, built from four physical cross-ratios. We also introduce a regulator in the double discontinuity operation and then prove that one subtraction suffices for all integrals to converge.

</details>


### [137] [Cosmological Correlators from Resurgence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20925)
*Yuanzhao Li,Zhong-Zhi Xianyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究从低能有效理论恢复被积掉的重粒子信息这一逆问题，在宇宙学暴胀背景下展示了如何重建包含重粒子交换的暴胀关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 积分掉重粒子通常被视为不可逆过程，会抹除截断能标以上的物理信息。但作者指出，在给定适当边界数据时，仍有可能从低能有效理论中恢复重粒子的传播自由度。经典例子是从重求和的Euler-Heisenberg有效作用量恢复Schwinger对产生。本文旨在将这一思路推广到宇宙学情境。

Method: 针对离散重粒子谱和任意树图拓扑，作者结合低能有效理论、幺正性以及Bunch-Davies边界条件来重建暴胀关联函数。对发散的EFT级数采用三种重求和方式：边界微分算符、谱表示和Borel重求和，并通过解析延拓恢复指数压低的宇宙对撞机信号。

Result: 成功证明了可以从低能有效理论配合幺正性和Bunch-Davies边界条件，重建包含重粒子交换的暴胀关联函数，并通过解析延拓恢复了指数压低的宇宙对撞机信号。

Conclusion: 该构造在局域EFT背景与非解析的重粒子产生之间建立了resurgent关系，也可视为色散bootstrap的一个逆问题。

Abstract: Integrating out heavy particles is generally viewed as an irreversible procedure that erases some physical information above the cutoff scale of the resulting effective theory. However, this does not preclude the possibility of recovering the heavy propagating degrees from the low-energy effective theory with appropriate boundary data supplied. A classic example is the recovery of Schwinger pair production from the resummed Euler-Heisenberg effective action. In this work, we perform a similar exercise in a cosmological setting. We show that it is possible to reconstruct the inflationary correlators with massive exchanges, for a discrete heavy spectrum and arbitrary tree topologies, from the low-energy effective theory together with unitarity and Bunch-Davies boundary conditions. The divergent EFT series is resummed in three ways: the boundary differential operators, spectral representation, and Borel resummation, and the exponentially suppressed cosmological collider signals are recovered via analytic continuation. Our construction provides a resurgent relation between the local EFT background and the nonanalytic heavy-particle production, and may also be viewed as an inverse problem for the dispersive bootstrap.

</details>


### [138] [Morse Bridge between Planar Kepler and Hyperbolic Landau Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01778)
*Mikhail S. Plyushchay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了平面Kepler–Coulomb系统与双曲Landau系统之间的精确对应：二者通过共同的Morse Hamiltonian、径向Liouville变换与耦合常数嬗变相联系，并构造了显式经典轨道映射及量子加权纠缠恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面Kepler–Coulomb（平直电动力学）与双曲Landau（弯曲磁动力学）表面上属于不同几何背景的物理系统。作者希望揭示二者共享的深层结构，寻找统一的约化框架与精确对应关系，以理解其经典轨道、辛结构与量子谱的联系。

Method: 采用共同的Morse Hamiltonian作为桥梁；利用带耦合常数嬗变的径向Liouville变换将分离的Kepler问题映射到Morse系统，并用以固定horocyclic动量的约化将双曲Landau问题映射到同一系统。在正则非径向扇区上构造直接经典轨道映射，结合约化Kepler辛形式与Binet选择的复结构定义Poincaré Kähler度量。量子层面通过复合量子Liouville映射消去Morse波函数，得到径向Kepler算子束与horocyclic Landau算子束之间的精确加权纠缠恒等式。

Result: 在共同归一化下，Coulomb耦合等于磁场与守恒horocyclic动量的带符号乘积，量子Landau约化产生普适半密度移位1/4。轨道映射将Landau高度对应为缩放的逆Kepler半径，把Kepler椭圆、抛物线与双曲线映射为磁圆、horocycle与hypercycle，并将Hamilton速端曲线仿射映射到Landau承载圆。所构造的Poincaré Kähler度量中，非退化Binet轨道具有等于逆偏心率的常数测地曲率，零耦合时退化为测地线；该度量与双曲Landau问题的Poincaré位形空间度量等距。在半整数磁场处，固定Kepler壳的角动量分层被重组为有限约化Landau塔及其阈值端点。

Conclusion: 尽管完整的母系统在幺正意义下不等价，但在所声明的约化扇区内，平面Kepler–Coulomb与双曲Landau系统之间存在精确的经典与量子对应。该工作统一了两类动力学，并为跨几何背景的可积系统约化提供了范例。

Abstract: A common Morse Hamiltonian bridges the planar Kepler--Coulomb and hyperbolic Landau systems, linking flat electric dynamics to curved magnetic dynamics. A radial Liouville transformation with coupling-constant metamorphosis maps the separated Kepler problem to the Morse system, while fixed-horocyclic-momentum reduction maps the hyperbolic Landau problem to the same system. In a common normalization, the Coulomb coupling is the signed product of the magnetic field and conserved horocyclic momentum, while the quantum Landau reduction has the universal half-density shift $1/4$. Beyond this shared reduction, we construct a direct classical orbit map on the regular nonradial sectors for all signs of the Kepler energy. It maps the Landau height to a scaled inverse Kepler radius, Kepler ellipses, parabolas and hyperbolas to magnetic circles, horocycles and hypercycles, and Hamilton's hodograph affinely to the Landau carrier circle. The reduced Kepler symplectic form and the Binet-selected complex structure determine a Poincaré Kähler metric. Its nondegenerate Binet orbits have constant geodesic curvature of magnitude equal to the inverse eccentricity, while zero coupling gives geodesics. The orbit map identifies this metric isometrically with the Poincaré configuration-space metric of the hyperbolic Landau problem. Composing the quantum Liouville maps eliminates the Morse wavefunction and yields an exact weighted intertwining identity between the radial Kepler and horocyclic Landau operator pencils. At distinguished half-integer magnetic fields, the angular-momentum grading of a fixed Kepler shell is reorganized into the finite reduced Landau tower and its threshold endpoint. These exact correspondences hold in the stated reduced sectors despite the unitary inequivalence of the complete parent systems.

</details>


### [139] [Vector Superradiance without Separability: Instability Rates from the Worldline Effective Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20960)
*Keegan Cove,Jingping Li,Riccardo Penco*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文用点粒子有效场论(EFT)研究超轻大质量矢量场在慢转黑洞周围的超辐射不稳定率，澄清了此前EFT与Proca方程结果之间表观的O(1)差异，并证明两者完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期有效场论给出与在固定黑洞背景上求解Proca方程所得超辐射不稳定率存在表观O(1)差异（如j=1、ℓ=2模式），且全理论计算依赖FKKS拟设的可分离性，限制了对缺乏隐藏对称性背景的推广。

Method: 采用点粒子有效场论，将引力子精确积掉后再按α展开；正确识别简并{ℓ=0, ℓ=2}子空间中的领头阶态；除远区外仅引入一个保守的世界线接触项，即视界在宇称偶j=1 Proca扇区中的静态单极响应，并通过与近区匹配确定该系数。

Result: EFT精确复现了全部j=1 Proca不稳定率，且无需借助FKKS拟设；该接触项同时将束缚态的精细结构劈裂修正到与已发表Proca谱完全一致，从而消除了文献中的表观张力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了矢量超辐射的简洁模型，表明不依赖可分离性的方法原则上可推广至缺乏隐藏对称性的旋转背景与天体；同时借以说明点粒子有效场论中涉及耗散效应的潜在技术细节。

Abstract: We use a point-particle effective field theory to study the superradiant instability rates of an ultralight massive vector around a slowly rotating black hole. Earlier effective-theory treatments reported an apparent $O(1)$ discrepancy with the rates obtained by solving the Proca equation on a fixed black-hole background, e.g. for the mode with $j=1$ and $\ell=2$. We show that there is in fact no discrepancy: once the leading-order states in the degenerate $\{\ell=0,\ell=2\}$ subspace are correctly identified, the effective theory (implemented here with the graviton integrated out exactly, and expanded in $α$ only afterwards) reproduces \emph{all} of the $j=1$ Proca instability rates exactly, and it does so \emph{without} ever invoking the FKKS ansatz on which the full-theory calculation rests. Since it never relies on separability, the same approach applies, at least in principle, to rotating backgrounds and bodies that lack the hidden symmetries underlying that ansatz. The single ingredient beyond the far zone is a conservative worldline contact term, the static monopole response of the horizon in the parity-even $j=1$ Proca sector, which we fix by matching to the near zone. The same term shifts the fine-structure splitting of the bound states into exact agreement with the published Proca spectrum. In addition to providing a conceptually straightforward model of vector superradiance and resolving an apparent tension in the literature, we present this work to illustrate potential technical subtleties involving dissipative effects in point particle effective theories.

</details>


### [140] [A boundary-addition identity for Weil-Petersson volumes and their supersymmetric generalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21030)
*Clifford V. Johnson*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在KdV可积层级框架下研究带测地边界的Riemann模空间的Weil-Petersson体积 $V_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$ 及其 $N\geq1$ 超对称推广。作者推导出一个精确的"边界添加恒等式"，将 $V_{g,n+1}$ 与背后的KdV数据直接联系，统一并推广了若干已知递归关系，给出新的几何解释与结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weil-Petersson体积及其超对称推广是模空间与相交理论的核心对象，已有若干零散的递归关系与结果（如Do-Norbury的结果）。作者希望找到一个统一的、由可积KdV层级支配的框架，将各类体积及其关系组织为一个完整层级，从而揭示其内在结构并获得新的恒等式与几何解释。

Method: 将 $V_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$ 及其 $N=1,2$ 和小 $N=4$ 超对称推广视为一个由KdV可积层级自然支配的更大族量的特化。推导精确的边界添加恒等式，直接联系 $V_{g,n+1}(\{b_1,...,b_n,b\})$（任意 $b$）与 $V_{g,n}$ 对应的 $n$-边界KdV数据；并对普通Weil-Petersson体积在可移除锥值 $b=2\pi i$ 附近展开，用附加KdV数据构造更高层级。

Result: 在 $b=2\pi i$ 附近展开复现了Do与Norbury的三个已知结果，并将其组织为完整层级的前几级，更高层级由附加KdV数据构建，并在相交理论中解释为 $ \kappa $-装饰体积之塔。该恒等式阐明了文献中若干其他递归关系并导出新关系；$N=1,2$ 及小 $N=4$ 超对称情形均被覆盖，展示了各自显著特性；并揭示了 $N=2$ 与小 $N=4$ Weil-Petersson体积的相交理论描述。

Conclusion: 边界添加恒等式为各类Weil-Petersson体积及其超对称推广提供了由KdV层级支配的统一框架，将已知结果统一为层级的前几级并自然生成更高层级，同时给出相交理论解释与若干新递归关系和体积的几何描述。

Abstract: We consider $V_{g,n}(\{b_i\})$, the Weil-Petersson volumes of the moduli space of Riemann surfaces of genus $g$ with $n$ geodesic boundaries of lengths $b_i$, $(i=1,...,n)$, and various ${ N}{\geq}1$ supersymmetric generalizations of them, using a framework within which they are specializations of a larger family of quantities naturally governed by the integrable KdV hierarchy. An exact boundary-addition identity is derived that directly relates $V_{g,n+1}(\{b_1,b_2,...,b_n,b\})$, for any $b$, to the corresponding $n$-boundary KdV data underlying $V_{g,n}(\{b_1,b_2,...,b_n\})$. For ordinary Weil-Petersson volumes, expansion about the special removable-cone value $b=2πi$ reproduces three known results of Do and Norbury, organizing them as the first levels of a complete hierarchy. The higher levels are naturally built from additional KdV data, which we interpret geometrically in intersection theory as a tower of $κ$-decorated volumes. Several other recursive relations in the literature are illuminated by the identity, and various new ones are derived. The cases with ${ N}=1,2$ and (small) ${N}=4$ supersymmetry are also covered by the identity, and we exhibit some of the striking special features and results arising in each case. Among many applications presented, we use the identity to uncover the intersection theory descriptions of ${N}=2$ and small ${ N}=4$ Weil-Petersson volumes.

</details>


### [141] [Diagnosing Inconsistencies in 4d N=1 Gauge Theories with Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21867)
*Seong-Jin Lee,Rak-Kyeong Seong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 利用CNN直接从Kasteleyn矩阵判别brane tilings的几何一致性，并用梯度显著性分析定位导致不一致的手征场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: brane tilings中只有满足几何一致性条件的图才对应良态量子场论，需高效判别一致性并定位缺陷来源。

Method: 对C^3/(Z_3×Z_3)轨道折叠的六边形面添加对角边构造4d N=1理论族，训练CNN从Kasteleyn矩阵判别几何一致性；对可通过Higgs掉单个双基本手征场恢复一致的tiling，用基于梯度的显著性分析定位责任手征场。

Result: CNN以高精度识别几何不一致；显著性分析定位责任手征场的精度显著高于匹配的随机基线。

Conclusion: 可解释AI(XAI)可用于识别超对称规范理论中导致不一致的局域缺陷。

Abstract: Brane tilings are bipartite graphs on a 2-torus that encode the Lagrangians of 4d N=1 supersymmetric gauge theories arising on D3-branes probing toric Calabi-Yau 3-folds. Among these bipartite graphs, only those that satisfy geometric consistency conditions correspond to well-behaved quantum field theories. We train a convolutional neural network (CNN) to distinguish geometrically consistent from inconsistent brane tilings directly from their Kasteleyn matrices. We study a family of 4d N=1 theories obtained by adding diagonal edges to the hexagonal faces of the brane tiling for the abelian orbifold C^3/Z_3 x Z_3, and find that the CNN identifies geometric inconsistency with high accuracy. For inconsistent brane tilings that can be rendered consistent by Higgsing a single bifundamental chiral field, we show that gradient-based saliency analysis can be used to localize the responsible chiral fields with accuracy well above a matched random baseline. These results demonstrate that explainable AI (XAI) can be used to identify local defects responsible for inconsistencies in supersymmetric gauge theories.

</details>


### [142] [On asymptotically and anomaly-free SU(N) chiral gauge theories for arbitrarily large N](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21129)
*Kort Beck,Patrick Draper*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了Eichten-Kang-Koh构造的一类具有大N极限、渐近自由且无反常的SU(N)手征规范理论的全局对称群结构，并系统列出了忠实对称性的 't Hooft 反常（含仅在分数磁通背景下可见的反常），提出利用分数磁通反常匹配作为约束红外行为的新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eichten、Kang和Koh构造了大量具有大N极限的渐近自由、无反常SU(N)手征规范理论目录，但这些理论的全局对称群结构及相应't Hooft反常尚未被系统计算，尤其是与分数磁通相关的反常对红外行为的约束作用未被充分探讨。

Method: 先用下降（descent）程序计算反常；再将理论置于四维环面（four-torus）上，利用背景规范场可携带扭曲磁通的性质做第二种计算。同时分析理论的忠实商（faithful quotient）如何产生分数磁通，并将其等效为单形式对称性的双形式背景来处理。

Result: 目录中大多数理论含基本荷物质，不存在真正的一形式电中心对称性，但分数磁通仍由忠实商产生并以与双形式背景相同的方式贡献反常；两类方法给出一致结果。据此系统列出了各理论的零形式与分数磁通相关反常，并讨论了若干候选红外行为。

Conclusion: 分数磁通反常匹配为手征规范理论的红外行为提供了一个超越普通零形式反常匹配的额外约束条件；基于大N推理，作者对一对模型提出了新的红外行为提案，该方案满足零形式't Hooft反常匹配，并额外通过了分数磁通匹配条件这一检验。

Abstract: A large catalog of asymptotically free and anomaly-free SU(N) chiral gauge theories that admit a large-N limit was constructed by Eichten, Kang, and Koh. Here we compute the global structure of their symmetry groups and tabulate the 't Hooft anomalies of the faithful symmetries, including those that are visible only in backgrounds carrying fractional flux. Most theories in the catalog contain fundamentally charged matter and support no genuine one-form electric center symmetry. However, fractional fluxes still arise from the faithful quotient and contribute to the anomaly in the same way as a two-form background for a one-form symmetry. The anomalies are computed using two methods, first with the descent procedure, and second by placing the theory on a four-torus, which supports twisted fluxes for background gauge fields. We discuss a selection of candidate IR behaviors and present a general discussion of how matching the anomalies associated with fractional flux constrains these theories beyond the imposition of ordinary zero-form anomaly matching. Based on large-N reasoning, we formulate a new proposal for the IR behavior of a pair of models, consistent with the zero-form 't Hooft anomaly matching conditions. The matching condition involving fractional flux amounts to one additional test, which the scenario is shown to pass.

</details>


### [143] [The Chern-Simons Function and the Quantum Hall Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21182)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义三维流形上规范联络的Chern-Simons不变量，并阐述其特殊性，重点说明其在量子霍尔效应及分数量子霍尔效应理解中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Chern-Simons不变量的特殊性质及其在凝聚态物理（量子霍尔效应、分数量子霍尔效应）中的关键作用。

Method: 在三维流形上定义规范联络的Chern-Simons不变量。

Result: 阐明Chern-Simons不变量在理解量子霍尔效应及其推广（分数量子霍尔效应）中的角色。

Conclusion: Chern-Simons不变量是连接拓扑与凝聚态物理中量子霍尔现象的重要工具。（本文基于2013年CUNY研究生中心James Simons 75岁生日会议讲座，将发表于《美国数学会公报》。）

Abstract: In this article, I will define the Chern-Simons invariant of a gauge connection over a three-manifold and explain some of the things that are special about it, in particular its role in understanding the quantum Hall effect, which is an important effect in condensed matter physics, and its generalization, the fractional quantum Hall effect. (The article is based on a lecture at a conference celebrating the 75th birthday of James Simons, held at the CUNY Graduate Center in 2013. It will appear in the Bulletin of the American Math Society.

</details>


### [144] [Entanglement entropy in holographic CFTs with generic boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21370)
*Fabio Ori,Peng-Xiang Hao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 全息共形场论中带边界的纠缠熵无法完全用实体时空对偶描述；在二维边界情形下，需将双时空延拓到复坐标才能与CFT预言精确匹配，RT面与膜等体对象一般为复但锚定在实渐近边界上，并用高斯费米子作数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有一般时空签名边界的全息共形场论的纠缠熵，无法完全纳入标准全息对偶（对应实的体时空）框架，需寻找更广义的对偶描述以精确重现场论结果。

Method: 在静态与含时两种设定下，将双体时空延拓至复坐标；考察Ryu-Takayanagi面与膜的复结构及其在实渐近边界上的锚定；并对二维自由高斯费米子体系进行第三种数值检验，覆盖不同纠缠相的渐近区域。

Result: 二维边界共形场论预言的精确匹配要求复坐标下的体时空；RT面与膜一般为复，但仍锚定在实渐近边界上，从而保持场论量的物理意义；自由高斯费米子的数值结果重现了相应渐近行为。

Conclusion: 标准全息对偶需推广到复体时空才能完整描述此类边界CFT的纠缠熵，物理可观测性由实边界锚定保证，并得到独立数值验证支持。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy in holographic conformal field theories with boundaries of generic spacetime signature cannot be described entirely within the standard holographic duality to a real bulk spacetime. We show, both in static and time-dependent setups, that an exact match with the conformal field theory predictions in two boundary dimensions entails an extension of the dual spacetime to complex coordinates. As a consequence, relevant bulk objects such as Ryu-Takayanagi surfaces and branes are in general complex, yet anchored on a real asymptotic boundary preserving the physical meaning of field-theoretical quantities. We also provide a third numerical check of the holographic prediction with a two-dimensional system of free Gaussian fermions exhibiting the same asymptotic regimes across different entanglement phases.

</details>


### [145] [A Differential Form Description of Partial Entanglement Entropy: Testing a Killing Vector Construction in Covariant Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21398)
*Chuanjia Zhu,Dong-Hui Du,Wen-Cong Gan,Fu-Wen Shu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入PEE thread流(部分纠缠熵线流)的微分形式描述，在真空AdS₃/CFT₂中验证其通量可复现已知纠缠轮廓；并尝试将PEE形式与协变相空间(CPS)构造的流相联系，发现以Killing矢量为基础的CPS构造无法复现中心PEE流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: bit thread(比特线)表述为全息纠缠熵提供了几何图像，而PEE及其PEE thread刻画了相对于单个边界点的结构。由于PEE thread流可叠加重构常规bit thread流，作者希望为PEE thread流建立微分形式描述，并探索其是否可由协变相空间(CPS)形式体系中的某种流给出，从而为PEE给出更基本的场论/引力解释。

Method: 1) 引入PEE thread流的微分形式描述；2) 在真空AdS₃/CFT₂中对一个区间计算该形式的通量，与已知纠缠轮廓对比以作一致性检验；3) 研究PEE形式是否能(至多相差一个恰当形式改进)与CPS形式体系构造的流相关联：选取Rindler-AdS₃中的精确Killing矢量，由参数a刻画背景族，将各背景的Killing矢量代入Iyer-Wald面荷形式并保留完整的a依赖，通过对a积分定义有限候选流；4) 针对中心边界点r₀=0处的反射对称PEE流，变换到Poincaré-AdS₃后检验是否可复现已知PEE流的两个分量。

Result: PEE形式通量成功复现了真空AdS₃/CFT₂区间情形的已知纠缠轮廓，验证了所提微分形式描述的自洽性。然而在CPS检验中，对于源自中心边界点r₀=0的反射对称PEE流，无论怎样选择Killing参数，变换到Poincaré-AdS₃后都无法同时复现已知PEE流的两个分量。

Conclusion: PEE thread流的微分形式描述在真空AdS₃/CFT₂中是自洽的。但所考虑的直接基于Killing矢量的CPS构造不足以复现中心PEE流。可能的推广方向包括：引入恰当形式改进、采用不同的CPS流，或选择更一般的生成元。

Abstract: The bit thread formulation provides a geometric description of holographic entanglement entropy, while partial entanglement entropy (PEE) and PEE threads resolve this structure with respect to individual boundary points. Motivated by the fact that PEE thread flows can be superposed to reconstruct conventional bit thread flows, we introduce a differential form description of PEE thread flow. For an interval in the vacuum AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ setup, we show that the flux of the resulting form reproduces the known entanglement contour, providing a consistency check of the proposed description. We then investigate whether the PEE form can be related, possibly up to an exact form improvement, to a current constructed from the covariant phase space (CPS) formalism. As a first test, we consider exact Killing vectors in Rindler-AdS$_3$. The Rindler parameter $a$ parametrizes a one-parameter family of backgrounds. At each value of $a$, the Killing vector of the corresponding background is substituted into the Iyer-Wald surface charge form, and the full $a$-dependence of the resulting form is retained. We define a finite candidate current by integrating this surface charge form over $a$. For the reflection-symmetric PEE flow sourced at the central boundary point $r_0=0$, we find that no choice of the Killing parameters reproduces both components of the known PEE flow after transforming to Poincaré-AdS$_3$. This result shows that the direct Killing vector CPS construction considered here is not sufficient to reproduce the central PEE flow. Possible extensions include an exact form improvement, a different CPS current, or a more general choice of generator.

</details>


### [146] [Complex scalar field thick branes: stability of linear perturbation and evolution of scalar Kaluza-Klein modes coupled with gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21421)
*Wang-Long Dong,Heng Guo,Qun Wei,Yong-Tao Lu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究复标量场产生的Minkowski、dS和AdS厚膜稳定性与局域化，并考察带曲率耦合的试探标量场KK模式与共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究含温度参数a的复标量场厚膜在三种背景下的稳定性、张量零模局域化，以及曲率耦合标量场KK模式与共振行为。

Method: 构造复标量场Φ的厚膜模型，分析标量、矢量、张量线性扰动及张量扰动方程分解；并引入动能项与曲率耦合F(R)的实试探标量场Ψ，研究其零模、KK谱与共振。

Result: a趋近临界值时Φ虚部呈双扭结、能量密度分裂为双峰形成双子膜；标量与矢量扇区无不稳定，张量无快子模。引力子零模在Minkowski和dS膜上局域，在AdS膜上不可归一。标量零模在Minkowski和dS膜可局域且KK谱连续无隙，临界a附近出现有质标量共振；增大β使共振增多并延长低阶共振寿命。AdS膜标量零模亦局域，但边界发散有效势将KK模束缚为束缚态，谱离散。

Conclusion: 该模型在Minkowski和dS膜上稳定且引力子/标量零模可局域；AdS膜引力子零模不可归一但标量零模局域且KK谱离散，共振性质受参数a与β调控。

Abstract: We investigate the stability and localization properties of Minkowski, de Sitter (dS), and anti-de Sitter (AdS) thick branes generated by a complex scalar field $Φ$. The scalar self-interaction potential contains a temperature parameter $a$. As $a$ approaches its critical value, the imaginary part of $Φ$ becomes double-kinked and the energy density splits into two peaks, indicating the formation of two sub-branes. The analysis of linear scalar, vector, and tensor perturbations shows no signs of instability in the scalar and vector sectors, while the factorization of the tensor perturbation equation excludes tachyonic tensor modes. The graviton zero mode is localized on the Minkowski and dS branes but is not normalizable on the AdS brane. We further study a real test scalar field $Ψ$ with a coupling function $F(R)$ between its kinetic term and the spacetime curvature. The scalar zero mode can always be localized on the Minkowski and dS branes, with a continuous gapless spectrum of scalar Kaluza--Klein (KK) modes. Massive scalar resonances emerge near the critical value of $a$ for suitable values of $β$. Increasing $β$ produces more resonances and prolongs the lifetimes of the lower resonances, while their time evolution exhibits the characteristic decay of metastable modes. For the AdS brane, the scalar zero mode is also localized, whereas the divergent effective potential at the boundaries traps the massive scalar KK modes as bound states, yielding a discrete mass spectrum.

</details>


### [147] [A Geodesic Route toward Holography beyond AdS: Tessellating the Schwarzschild Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21471)
*Shi Gu,Qiang Wen,Haocheng Zhong,Yiwei Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将真空AdS中的测地线镶嵌推广到Schwarzschild黑洞及更一般的Riemann流形，构建了基于Crofton重构的PEE张量网络全息模型，并实现了Bekenstein-Hawking熵、RT公式与ER=EPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真空AdS可由测地线网络完美镶嵌，并据此定义AdS/CFT的部分纠缠熵（PEE）张量网络玩具模型，但该构造能否推广到非真空AdS背景此前尚不清楚。

Method: 从边界发射特定测地线气体，对Schwarzschild黑洞外部区域及其Einstein-Rosen桥进行完美镶嵌；证明该测地线镶嵌及由此定义的Crofton重构可推广到更一般的Riemann流形；据此构建更一般几何背景上的PEE张量网络全息模型。

Result: 在Schwarzschild背景的PEE张量网络模型中，具体实现了Bekenstein-Hawking熵、Ryu-Takayanagi公式和ER=EPR提议。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将测地线镶嵌与Crofton重构推广至非真空及更一般几何背景，为超越AdS/CFT的全息玩具模型开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In vacuum anti-de Sitter (AdS) space, the geometry admits a perfect tessellation by a geodesic network. This tessellation characterizes the background exactly, and a partial entanglement entropy (PEE) tensor-network toy model of AdS/CFT can be defined on it. It has so far been unclear whether this construction can be extended beyond vacuum AdS. In this paper we take the first step in this direction. We show that the exterior region of the Schwarzschild black hole and its Einstein--Rosen bridge are perfectly tessellated by a specific geodesic gas emitted from the boundary, so that Crofton reconstruction holds in these regions. We then prove that such a geodesic tessellation and the Crofton reconstruction it defines extend to more generic Riemannian manifolds. This allows us to construct a PEE tensor-network model of holographic duality on more generic geometric backgrounds. In the case of the PEE tensor-network model for the Schwarzschild background, we can concretely realize the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy, the Ryu--Takayanagi formula and the ER=EPR proposal. Our approach opens a new route towards holographic toy models beyond AdS/CFT.

</details>


### [148] [BMS$_3$ modules from path integrals on the coadjoint orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21691)
*Pujian Mao,Xin-Cheng Mao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过单圈路径积分量子化，系统构造了渐近对称群余伴随轨道对应的量子模，并用几何作用量路径积分确定参考态，给出Virasoro与BMS$_3$群各常轨道的余伴随模及特征标，结果与周期欧氏方向路径积分一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近对称群的余伴随轨道在引力与共形场论中具有重要意义，但其量子模的系统构造及收敛性（尤其哈密顿量无下界的轨道）尚缺乏统一处理。本文旨在通过路径积分量子化建立余伴随轨道的量子模构造框架。

Method: 采用单圈路径积分量子化：对哈密顿量无下界的轨道，选取适当积分围道，定义收敛的欧氏半线路径积分；以几何作用量在余伴随轨道上的路径积分确定与轨道关联的参考态，并由参考态及其后代张成余伴随模。将该形式应用于Virasoro群和BMS$_3$群，构造所有常轨道对应的余伴随模，并通过模内态计数计算特征标。

Result: 给出了Virasoro与BMS$_3$群所有常轨道对应的余伴随模；计算出各常轨道的特征标，与周期欧氏方向下几何作用量路径积分的结果一致。

Conclusion: 单圈路径积分量子化为渐近对称群余伴随轨道提供了系统的量子模构造方法，半线收敛路径积分与参考态选取方案可行，且模的特征标与几何作用量路径积分互相印证，支持该框架的合理性。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a systematic construction of quantum modules associated with coadjoint orbits of asymptotic symmetry groups through the path integral quantization at one-loop order. In particular, for the orbits with Hamiltonians unbounded from below, we show that an appropriate choice of integration contour defines a convergent Euclidean half-line path integral. The reference state associate with the orbit is determined by the path integral of the geometric action on the corresponding coadjoint orbit. The coadjoint module is therefore spanned by the reference state and its descendants. We apply this formalism to the Virasoro and BMS$_3$ groups and derive the coadjoint modules corresponding to all constant orbits. We also compute the characters of all constant Virasoro and BMS$_3$ coadjoint orbits by counting the states within the modules, and the results agree with the corresponding computations from the path integral of the geometric action with a periodic Euclidean direction.

</details>


### [149] [Electroweak balls: non-topological solitons in the Weinberg-Salam theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19293)
*Carlos Herdeiro,Jutta Kunz,Burkhard Kleihaus,Eugen Radu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在玻色SU(2)×U(1) Weinberg-Salam理论中构造了一类新的光滑有限能量孤子——电弱球，其局域化机制类似Q-ball，带电矢量场具有简谐时间依赖而能量-动量张量保持定态。作者显式构造了球对称电型解与轴对称磁型解，发现解族具有有限频率区间、质量间隙及双分支结构，并指出该构造不依赖于标准模型实测参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Q-ball的局域化思想推广到规范矢量场情形，构造Proca-Higgs balls的电弱对应物，其中矢量玻色子质量由希格斯机制产生而非人为引入，从而拓展对非拓扑孤子及电弱理论非线性结构的理解。

Method: 在玻色SU(2)×U(1) Weinberg-Salam理论中引入矢量场的简谐时间依赖以保持能量-动量张量定态，进而数值/解析构造球对称电型解与轴对称磁型解，并分析解族随频率变化的谱结构。

Result: 成功构造出光滑有限能量的电弱球解，包含球对称电型与轴对称磁型两类；解族呈现有限频率区间、质量间隙及双分支结构；该构造可推广至不同规范耦合、希格斯自耦合和对称破缺标度的电弱型扇区，但在标准模型实测耦合与质量比下未找到解。

Conclusion: 电弱球是Proca-Higgs balls在电弱理论中的自然对应物，其存在不限于标准模型参数，为研究不同参数区间下的孤子质量与粒子质量标度提供了新框架；标准模型实测参数下不存在此类解。

Abstract: We construct a new class of smooth, finite-energy solitons in the bosonic $SU(2)\times U(1)$ Weinberg-Salam theory, which we dub $electroweak$ $balls$. Their localization mechanism is analogous in spirit to that of $Q$-balls: the charged vector fields possess a harmonic time dependence while the energy-momentum tensor remains time independent. We explicitly construct both spherically symmetric electric-type solutions and axisymmetric magnetic-type solutions, and show that they form families characterized by a finite frequency interval, a mass gap, and a two-branch structure. These configurations provide electroweak counterparts of Proca-Higgs balls, with the vector-boson masses generated by the Higgs mechanism rather than introduced explicitly. The construction is not tied to the measured parameters of the Standard Model. More generally, it applies to bosonic electroweak-type sectors with different gauge couplings, Higgs self-coupling and symmetry-breaking scale, and hence potentially very different characteristic particle and soliton mass scales. For the families studied here, we do not find solutions at the measured Standard Model couplings and mass ratios.

</details>


### [150] [A Split-Signature Type IIB Background on $\mathrm{AdS}_{5,\mathbb C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21783)
*Brenno Carlini Vallilo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在复AdS₅底流形T*S⁵上构造了(5,5)号差的最大超对称IIB背景，导出SL(4,ℂ)不变度量与五形式，满足自对偶与Einstein方程，具有32个实Killing旋量，超代数对应𝔭𝔰𝔩(4|4,ℂ)的实形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索最大超对称IIB背景在非洛伦兹（(5,5)号差）情形下的存在性与结构，拓展对超引力背景及超共形代数实形式的理解。

Method: 在复AdS₅的实十维流形T*S⁵上，采用最一般的SL(4,ℂ)不变度量与五形式拟设，要求自对偶性与Einstein方程固定相位，利用全纯余框架显式计算超协变曲率抵消，并通过平坦输运求解Killing旋量。

Result: 得到标量平坦非Einstein度量G=i(q̄−q)与自对偶实五形式F₅，符合无扭曲IIB₅理论的实条件；存在32个全局实Killing旋量，其Killing超代数为𝔭𝔰𝔩(4|4,ℂ)的因子交换实形式，并识别出相应的超陪集。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了(5,5)号差下的最大超对称IIB背景，揭示了其超共形结构与超陪集描述，为混合号差超引力背景研究提供了具体范例。

Abstract: We construct a maximally supersymmetric Type IIB background in signature $(5,5)$ on the real ten-manifold underlying complex $\mathrm{AdS}_5$. The spacetime is diffeomorphic to $T^*S^5$ and carries the invariant metric $G=\mathrm i(\bar q-q)$, where $q$ is the holomorphic constant-curvature metric. The real five-form $F_5=(4\mathrm i/L)(Ω-\barΩ)$, built from its normalized volume form, is self-dual, and its stress tensor supports this scalar-flat, non-Einstein metric. We derive these fields from the most general $\mathrm{SL}(4,\mathbb C)$-invariant metric and five-form ansatz: self-duality and the Einstein equation fix their phases up to the overall scale, orientation, and flux sign. The fields obey the reality conditions of the untwisted $\mathrm{IIB}_5$ theory. The holomorphic coframe makes the supercovariant curvature cancellation explicit, and flat transport gives thirty-two global real Killing spinors. Their Killing superalgebra is the factor-exchanging real form of $\mathfrak{psl}(4|4,\mathbb C)$, and we identify the corresponding supercoset.

</details>


### [151] [Multi-Boundary Spinning $AdS_5$ Black Holes, Strongly Coupled Plasma Balls and a dual locally de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22084)
*Andrés Anabalón,Guillermo Buckle,Annelies Fullgraff,Marcelo Oyarzo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 构造了五维负宇宙学常数广义相对论中一类新的精确旋转黑洞解，其对偶为四维闵可夫斯基时空上的强耦合旋转等离子体球；边界洛伦兹因子作为体时空新坐标，产生局部为dS₃×ℝ的新UV区域，对偶能动张量为满足弱、强能量条件的共形各向异性流体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在四维体时空旋转黑孔解（边界洛伦兹因子充当体坐标并给出新UV区域）被发现的背景下，将该构造推广至五维，以研究其全息对偶的旋转等离子体球及新UV区域的物理性质。

Method: 在五维广义相对论中引入负宇宙学常数，采用AdS/CFT对偶构造精确旋转黑洞解；将边界洛伦兹因子提升为体时空坐标，分析所产生的新UV区域的几何结构（dS₃×ℝ），并计算对偶能动张量。

Result: 获得一族五维精确旋转黑洞解，对偶于四维闵可夫斯基时空上的强耦合旋转等离子体球；新UV区域局部为三维de Sitter时空与实直线的乘积；对偶能动张量对应共形各向异性流体，并满足弱能量条件与强能量条件。

Conclusion: 五维旋转黑孔解的同构构造可行，边界洛伦兹因子作为体坐标产生新UV区域这一机制在更高维推广成立；该UV区域的对偶流体为健康的共形各向异性流体，为进一步研究旋转等离子体与全息流体性质提供了精确引力对偶框架。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact spinning black hole solutions in five-dimensional General Relativity with a negative cosmological constant, which are dual to strongly coupled spinning plasma balls on four-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. In analogy with the recent discovery of such black solutions in four bulk dimensions, the boundary Lorentz factor acts as a new coordinate in the bulk, giving rise to a new UV region that, in our case, is locally a three-dimensional de Sitter spacetime times the real line. The dual energy-momentum tensor in this region describes a conformal anisotropic fluid that satisfies the weak and strong energy conditions.

</details>
